File Summary
================================================================

Purpose:
--------
This file contains a packed representation of a subset of the repository's contents that is considered the most important context.
It is designed to be easily consumable by AI systems for analysis, code review,
or other automated processes.

File Format:
------------
The content is organized as follows:
1. This summary section
2. Repository information
3. Directory structure
4. Repository files (if enabled)
5. Multiple file entries, each consisting of:
  a. A separator line (================)
  b. The file path (File: path/to/file)
  c. Another separator line
  d. The full contents of the file
  e. A blank line

Usage Guidelines:
-----------------
- This file should be treated as read-only. Any changes should be made to the
  original repository files, not this packed version.
- When processing this file, use the file path to distinguish
  between different files in the repository.
- Be aware that this file may contain sensitive information. Handle it with
  the same level of security as you would the original repository.

Notes:
------
- Some files may have been excluded based on .gitignore rules and Repomix's configuration
- Binary files are not included in this packed representation. Please refer to the Repository Structure section for a complete list of file paths, including binary files
- Files matching these patterns are excluded: node_modules/**, dist/**, .git/**, *.log, *.mp4, *.webp, *.png, *.jpg, *.jpeg, *.gif
- Files matching patterns in .gitignore are excluded
- Files matching default ignore patterns are excluded
- Files are sorted by Git change count (files with more changes are at the bottom)
- Git diffs from the worktree and staged changes are included
- Git logs (20 commits) are included to show development patterns

const DISCOVERY_MEDIA_KEYS = ["tasteUmami", "tasteSweet", "tasteFloral", "tasteDaily"] as const satisfies readonly HomepageMediaKey[];
const RITUAL_MEDIA_KEYS = ["ritualSift", "ritualWater", "ritualWhisk", "ritualEnjoy", "tasteDaily"] as const satisfies readonly HomepageMediaKey[];
const JOURNAL_MEDIA_KEYS = ["spectrumBowl", "heroOrigin", "storyBowl", "tasteDaily"] as const satisfies readonly HomepageMediaKey[];
const SCORE_KEYS: ScoreKey[] = ["body_score", "umami_score", "sweetness_score", "bitterness_score"];

function scoreAverage(products: TeaProduct[], key: ScoreKey): number | null {
  const values = products.map((product) => product[key]).filter((value): value is number => typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value));
  return values.length ? values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / values.length : null;
}

function getSummary(product: TeaProduct, vi: boolean) {
  return (vi ? product.tasting_summary_vi : product.tasting_summary_en) || (vi ? product.description_vi : product.description_en) || "";
}

function formatPrice(product: TeaProduct, vi: boolean): string | null {
  if (typeof product.price !== "number") return null;
  const formatted = new Intl.NumberFormat(vi ? "vi-VN" : "en-US").format(product.price);
  return product.price_unit ? `${formatted}đ / ${product.price_unit}` : `${formatted}đ`;
}

function toMedia(slot: HomepageMediaSlot, vi: boolean): MediaItem {
  return { url: slot.url, alt: vi ? slot.altVi : slot.altEn, position: slot.position };
}

function scorePosition(score: number) {
  return Math.min(100, Math.max(0, ((score - 1) / 4) * 100));
}

function scalarMessages(copy: Copy) {
  const { discoveryCards: _a, scoreLabels: _b, ritualSteps: _c, journalCards: _d, ...messages } = copy;
  return messages;
}

export async function HomePage() {
  const [{ lng }, media, brands, matcha] = await Promise.all([
    getT("common"),
    getHomepageMedia(),
    getTeaBrands(),
    getTeaProducts({ teaType: "matcha" }),
  ]);

  const locale: LocaleCode = lng === "vi" || lng.startsWith("vi-") ? "vi" : "en";
  const vi = locale === "vi";
  const copy = COPY[locale];
  const featured = getHomepageFeaturedMatcha(matcha, 6);
  const scoreItems = featured.length ? featured : matcha.slice(0, 6);
  const visibleBrands = brands.filter((brand) => brand.is_visible).slice(0, 6);

  const products: HomeExperienceProps["products"] = featured.map((product) => {
    const image = resolveProductMedia(product);
    return {
      id: product.id,
      name: product.name,
      japaneseName: product.japanese_name || null,
      brand: product.brand?.name || "Japanese Tea",
      summary: getSummary(product, vi),
      price: formatPrice(product, vi),
      origin: product.origin_region || product.origin_country || "Japan",
      href: withLocalePath(`/matcha/${product.slug}`, lng),
      image: image ? { src: image.src, alt: vi ? image.altVi : image.altEn, fit: image.fit, position: image.position } : null,
    };
  });

  const tasteItems: HomeExperienceProps["tasteItems"] = copy.discoveryCards.map((card, index) => ({
    ...card,
    href: withLocalePath("/matcha", lng),
    image: toMedia(media[DISCOVERY_MEDIA_KEYS[index]], vi),
  }));

  const spectrum: HomeExperienceProps["spectrum"] = SCORE_KEYS.map((key) => {
    const score = scoreAverage(scoreItems, key);
    const labels = copy.scoreLabels[key];
    return { key, ...labels, score, position: score === null ? 50 : scorePosition(score) };
  });

  const ritual: HomeExperienceProps["ritual"] = copy.ritualSteps.map((step, index) => ({
    ...step,
    image: toMedia(media[RITUAL_MEDIA_KEYS[index]], vi),
  }));

  const journal: HomeExperienceProps["journal"] = copy.journalCards.map((card, index) => ({
    ...card,
    href: withLocalePath("/guide", lng),
    image: toMedia(media[JOURNAL_MEDIA_KEYS[index]], vi),
  }));

  const props: HomeExperienceProps = {
    locale,
    links: {
      matcha: withLocalePath("/matcha", lng),
      brands: withLocalePath("/brands", lng),
      guide: withLocalePath("/guide", lng),
    },
    hero: { image: toMedia(media.heroMain, vi), detail: toMedia(media.heroOrigin, vi) },
    products,
    tasteItems,
    spectrum,
    brands: visibleBrands.map((brand) => ({ id: brand.id, name: brand.name, japaneseName: brand.japanese_name || null, origin: brand.origin_region || brand.origin_country || "Japan", href: withLocalePath(`/brands/${brand.slug}`, lng) })),
    culture: { landscape: toMedia(media.storyLandscape, vi), detail: toMedia(media.spectrumBowl, vi), tool: toMedia(media.ritualPoster, vi) },
    ritual,
    journal,
    journalHero: toMedia(media.journalFeature, vi),
  };

  return (
    <main className={styles.root}>
      <NextIntlClientProvider locale={locale} messages={{ Home: scalarMessages(copy) }}>
        <SmoothScrollProvider>
          <HomeExperience {...props} />
        </SmoothScrollProvider>
      </NextIntlClientProvider>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import type { CSSProperties } from "react";
import { useLayoutEffect, useMemo, useRef } from "react";
import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ArrowDown, ArrowUpRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useTranslations } from "next-intl";
import { useKeenSlider } from "keen-slider/react";
import "keen-slider/keen-slider.min.css";
import gsap from "gsap";
import { ScrollTrigger } from "gsap/ScrollTrigger";
import type { HomeExperienceProps } from "./types";
import styles from "../HomePage.module.css";

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

const fmt = (value: number) => String(value).padStart(2, "0");

export default function HomeExperience(props: HomeExperienceProps) {
  const t = useTranslations("Home");
  const rootRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const heroRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const heroMediaRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const featuredRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const horizontalTrackRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const tasteRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const tastePinRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const ritualRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const ritualPinRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const journalRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);

  const [productSliderRef] = useKeenSlider<HTMLDivElement>({
    renderMode: "performance",
    mode: "free-snap",
    rubberband: true,
    slides: { perView: 1.16, spacing: 14 },
    breakpoints: {
      "(min-width: 640px)": { slides: { perView: 2.15, spacing: 18 } },
      "(min-width: 900px)": { slides: { perView: 2.85, spacing: 20 } },
    },
  });

  const [journalSliderRef] = useKeenSlider<HTMLDivElement>({
    renderMode: "performance",
    mode: "free-snap",
    slides: { perView: 1.08, spacing: 12 },
    breakpoints: {
      "(min-width: 640px)": { slides: { perView: 2.1, spacing: 16 } },
      "(min-width: 900px)": { slides: { perView: 3.1, spacing: 18 } },
    },
  });

  const productCountLabel = useMemo(
    () => `${fmt(props.products.length)} / ${t("archiveCount")}`,
    [props.products.length, t]
  );

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const root = rootRef.current;
    if (!root) return;

    const mm = gsap.matchMedia();
    const ctx = gsap.context(() => {
      mm.add("(prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)", () => {
        const heroTitleLines = gsap.utils.toArray<HTMLElement>("[data-hero-title] > span", root);
        const heroTl = gsap.timeline({ defaults: { ease: "power3.out" } });

        heroTl
          .fromTo("[data-hero-kicker]", { y: 18 }, { y: 0, duration: 0.55 }, 0.04)
          .fromTo(heroTitleLines, { yPercent: 82 }, { yPercent: 0, stagger: 0.09, duration: 1.05 }, 0.08)
          .fromTo("[data-hero-lead]", { y: 18 }, { y: 0, duration: 0.72 }, 0.28)
          .fromTo("[data-hero-cta]", { y: 12 }, { y: 0, duration: 0.62 }, 0.38)
          .fromTo(heroMediaRef.current, { scale: 1.045 }, { scale: 1, duration: 1.35, ease: "power2.out" }, 0);

        gsap.to("[data-seal]", {
          rotate: 360,
          duration: 38,
          ease: "none",
          repeat: -1,
        });

        gsap.to("[data-hero-float]", {
          yPercent: 7,
          scale: 1.035,
          ease: "none",
          scrollTrigger: {
            trigger: heroRef.current,
            start: "top top",
            end: "bottom top",
            scrub: 0.85,
          },
        });

        gsap.to("[data-hero-copy]", {
          yPercent: -10,
          ease: "none",
          scrollTrigger: {
            trigger: heroRef.current,
            start: "20% top",
            end: "bottom top",
            scrub: 0.8,
          },
        });

        const revealTargets = gsap.utils.toArray<HTMLElement>("[data-reveal]", root);
        revealTargets.forEach((node) => {
          gsap.fromTo(
            node,
            { y: 22 },
            {
              y: 0,
              duration: 0.82,
              ease: "power2.out",
              scrollTrigger: {
                trigger: node,
                start: "top 90%",
                once: true,
              },
            }
          );
        });

        const cultureMain = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>("[data-culture-main]");
        if (cultureMain) {
          gsap.fromTo(
            cultureMain,
            { yPercent: 4, scale: 1.035 },
            {
              yPercent: -4,
              scale: 1.01,
              ease: "none",
              scrollTrigger: {
                trigger: cultureMain,
                start: "top bottom",
                end: "bottom top",
                scrub: 0.9,
              },
            }
          );
        }

        if (journalRef.current) {
          gsap.to("[data-journal-image]", {
            yPercent: -5,
            scale: 1.015,
            ease: "none",
            scrollTrigger: {
              trigger: journalRef.current,
              start: "top bottom",
              end: "bottom top",
              scrub: 0.85,
            },
          });
        }
      });

      mm.add("(min-width: 1101px) and (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)", () => {
        const featured = featuredRef.current;
        const track = horizontalTrackRef.current;

        if (featured && track) {
          const cards = gsap.utils.toArray<HTMLElement>("[data-archive-card]", track);
          cards.forEach((card, index) => {
            gsap.set(card, { y: index % 3 === 1 ? 54 : index % 3 === 2 ? 18 : 0 });
          });

          const getDistance = () => Math.max(0, track.scrollWidth - featured.clientWidth + window.innerWidth * 0.2);

          gsap.to(track, {
            x: () => -getDistance(),
            ease: "none",
            scrollTrigger: {
              trigger: featured,
              start: "top top",
              end: () => `+=${Math.max(1100, getDistance() + window.innerWidth * 0.82)}`,
              pin: true,
              scrub: 0.85,
              anticipatePin: 1,
              invalidateOnRefresh: true,
            },
          });
        }

        if (tasteRef.current && tastePinRef.current) {
          const rows = gsap.utils.toArray<HTMLElement>("[data-spectrum-row]", tastePinRef.current);
          const tasteItems = gsap.utils.toArray<HTMLElement>("[data-taste-item]", tastePinRef.current);
          const tasteTl = gsap.timeline({
            scrollTrigger: {
              trigger: tasteRef.current,
              start: "top top",
              end: "+=1800",
              pin: tastePinRef.current,
              scrub: 0.75,
              anticipatePin: 1,
            },
          });

          tasteTl.fromTo("[data-taste-copy]", { y: 36 }, { y: 0, duration: 0.6 }, 0);

          tasteItems.forEach((item, index) => {
            tasteTl.fromTo(
              item,
              { x: 18, opacity: 0.45 },
              { x: 0, opacity: 1, duration: 0.34 },
              0.18 + index * 0.14
            );
          });

          rows.forEach((row, index) => {
            const dot = row.querySelector<HTMLElement>("[data-axis-dot]");
            const fill = row.querySelector<HTMLElement>("[data-axis-fill]");
            tasteTl.fromTo(row, { opacity: 0.38 }, { opacity: 1, duration: 0.38 }, 0.26 + index * 0.22);
            if (fill) tasteTl.fromTo(fill, { scaleX: 0 }, { scaleX: 1, duration: 0.5 }, 0.32 + index * 0.22);
            if (dot) tasteTl.fromTo(dot, { scale: 0.45 }, { scale: 1, duration: 0.3 }, 0.45 + index * 0.22);
          });
        }

        if (ritualRef.current && ritualPinRef.current) {
          const cards = gsap.utils.toArray<HTMLElement>("[data-ritual-step]", ritualPinRef.current);
          const ritualTl = gsap.timeline({
            scrollTrigger: {
              trigger: ritualRef.current,
              start: "top top",
              end: "+=1750",
              pin: ritualPinRef.current,
              scrub: 0.8,
              anticipatePin: 1,
            },
          });

          cards.forEach((card, index) => {
            ritualTl.to(cards, { opacity: 0.2, duration: 0.16 }, index === 0 ? 0 : ">");
            ritualTl.to(card, { opacity: 1, x: -8, duration: 0.34 }, "<");
          });
        }
      });
    }, root);

    return () => {
      mm.revert();
      ctx.revert();
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div ref={rootRef} className={styles.experience}>
      <section ref={heroRef} className={styles.heroScene} aria-labelledby="home-title">
        <div className={styles.heroPaper} aria-hidden="true" />
        <div className={styles.heroGrid}>
          <div className={styles.heroCopy} data-hero-copy="true">
            <p className={styles.eyebrow} data-hero-kicker="true">{t("heroEyebrow")}</p>
            <h1 id="home-title" className={styles.heroTitle} data-hero-title="true">
              <span>{t("heroLine1")}</span>
              <span>{t("heroLine2")}</span>
            </h1>
            <p className={styles.heroLead} data-hero-lead="true">{t("heroDescription")}</p>
            <div className={styles.heroActions} data-hero-cta="true">
              <Link href={props.links.matcha} className={styles.primaryCta}>
                {t("explore")}<ArrowUpRight size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
              </Link>
              <Link href={props.links.brands} className={styles.ghostLink}>
                {t("originLink")}<ArrowUpRight size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
              </Link>
            </div>
            <div className={styles.heroMeta}>
              <span>{t("heroMetaOne")}</span><i />
              <span>{t("heroMetaTwo")}</span><i />
              <span>{t("heroMetaThree")}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div ref={heroMediaRef} className={styles.heroMedia} data-hero-float="true">
            <Image
              src={props.hero.image.url}
              alt={props.hero.image.alt}
              fill
              priority
              quality={90}
              sizes="(max-width: 720px) 100vw, (max-width: 1100px) 64vw, 58vw"
              className={styles.heroImage}
              style={{ objectPosition: props.hero.image.position }}
            />
            <div className={styles.heroFrame} aria-hidden="true" />
            <div className={styles.heroJapanese} aria-hidden="true">一服、より良い日々へ</div>
            <div className={styles.heroSeal} data-seal="true" aria-hidden="true">
              <span>MIE</span><b>抹茶</b><small>ARCHIVE</small>
            </div>
            <figure className={styles.heroDetail}>
              <Image
                src={props.hero.detail.url}
                alt={props.hero.detail.alt}
                fill
                quality={75}
                sizes="180px"
                className={styles.coverImage}
                style={{ objectPosition: props.hero.detail.position }}
              />

          </div>
        </div>
        <div className={styles.heroScrollCue} aria-hidden="true">
          <span>{t("scroll")}</span><ArrowDown size={13} />

      </section>

      <section ref={featuredRef} className={styles.archiveStory} aria-labelledby="archive-title">
        <div className={styles.archiveHead}>
          <div>
            <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{t("archiveEyebrow")}</p>
            <h2 id="archive-title">{t("archiveTitle")}</h2>
          </div>
          <div className={styles.archiveIntro}>
            <p>{t("archiveDescription")}</p>
            <span>{productCountLabel}</span>
          </div>

        <div className={styles.archiveDesktopViewport}>

            {props.products.map((product, index) => (
              <article className={styles.archiveCard} key={product.id} data-archive-card="true">
                <Link href={product.href} className={styles.archiveCardLink}>
                  <span className={styles.archiveIndex}>{fmt(index + 1)}</span>
                  <figure className={styles.archiveProductMedia}>
                    {product.image ? (

                        src={product.image.src}

                        style={{ objectPosition: product.image.position }}

                    <span className={styles.archiveHalo} aria-hidden="true" />

                  <div className={styles.archiveCardCopy}>
                    <p>{product.brand}</p>
                    <div><h3>{product.name}</h3><ArrowUpRight size={15} aria-hidden="true" /></div>
                    {product.summary ? <p>{product.summary}</p> : null}
                    <footer><span>{product.price || product.origin}</span><small>{product.japaneseName}</small></footer>
                  </div>
                </Link>
              </article>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div ref={productSliderRef} className={`keen-slider ${styles.archiveMobileSlider}`}>
          {props.products.map((product, index) => (
            <article className={`keen-slider__slide ${styles.archiveSlide}`} key={product.id}>
              <Link href={product.href} className={styles.archiveCardLink}>
                <span className={styles.archiveIndex}>{fmt(index + 1)}</span>
                <figure className={styles.archiveProductMedia}>
                  {product.image ? (
                    <Image src={product.image.src} alt={product.image.alt} fill quality={75} sizes="78vw" className={product.image.fit === "contain" ? styles.productContain : styles.coverImage} style={{ objectPosition: product.image.position }} />
                  ) : <span className={styles.archiveFallback}>{product.japaneseName || product.name}</span>}
                </figure>
                <div className={styles.archiveCardCopy}><p>{product.brand}</p><div><h3>{product.name}</h3><ArrowUpRight size={15} /></div>{product.summary ? <p>{product.summary}</p> : null}</div>
              </Link>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section ref={tasteRef} className={styles.tasteStory} aria-labelledby="taste-title">
        <div ref={tastePinRef} className={styles.tastePinned}>
          <div className={styles.tasteCopy} data-taste-copy="true">
            <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{t("tasteEyebrow")}</p>
            <h2 id="taste-title">{t("tasteTitle")}</h2>
            <p>{t("tasteDescription")}</p>
            <div className={styles.tasteKanji} aria-hidden="true">味</div>
          </div>

          <ul className={styles.tasteIndex}>
            {props.tasteItems.map((item, index) => (
              <li key={item.title} data-reveal="true" data-taste-item="true">
                <Link href={item.href} className={styles.tasteIndexLink}>
                  <figure><Image src={item.image.url} alt={item.image.alt} fill quality={75} sizes="88px" className={styles.coverImage} style={{ objectPosition: item.image.position }} /></figure>
                  <div><span>{fmt(index + 1)}</span><h3>{item.title}</h3><p>{item.description}</p></div>
                  <ArrowUpRight size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
                </Link>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>

          <figure className={styles.spectrumPlate}>
            <figcaption><span>味</span><div><p className={styles.eyebrow}>{t("spectrumEyebrow")}</p><h3>{t("spectrumTitle")}</h3></div></figcaption>
            <dl>
              {props.spectrum.map((axis) => (
                <div className={styles.spectrumRow} key={axis.key} data-spectrum-row="true">
                  <dt><strong>{axis.title}</strong><span>{axis.low} — {axis.high}</span></dt>
                  <dd>
                    <div className={styles.axisShell}>
                      <span className={styles.axisFill} data-axis-fill="true" style={{ "--axis-x": `${axis.position}%` } as CSSProperties} />
                      {axis.score === null ? <span className={styles.axisMissing} data-axis-dot="true" /> : <span className={styles.axisDot} data-axis-dot="true" style={{ "--axis-x": `${axis.position}%` } as CSSProperties} />}
                    </div>
                    <div className={styles.axisLabels}><span>{axis.low}</span><span>{axis.high}</span></div>
                    <span className={styles.axisValue}>{axis.score === null ? t("spectrumEmpty") : `${axis.score.toFixed(1)} / 5`}</span>
                  </dd>
                </div>
              ))}
            </dl>
          </figure>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className={styles.cultureSection} aria-labelledby="culture-title">
        <div className={styles.cultureMedia} data-reveal="true">
          <figure className={styles.cultureMain} data-culture-main="true">
            <Image src={props.culture.landscape.url} alt={props.culture.landscape.alt} fill quality={90} sizes="(max-width: 1100px) 100vw, 58vw" className={styles.coverImage} style={{ objectPosition: props.culture.landscape.position }} />
          </figure>
          <figure className={styles.cultureInset}>
            <Image src={props.culture.detail.url} alt={props.culture.detail.alt} fill quality={75} sizes="240px" className={styles.coverImage} style={{ objectPosition: props.culture.detail.position }} />
          </figure>
        </div>
        <div className={styles.cultureCopy} data-reveal="true" data-culture-copy="true">
          <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{t("cultureEyebrow")}</p>
          <h2 id="culture-title">{t("cultureTitle")}</h2>
          <p>{t("cultureDescription")}</p>
          <Link href={props.links.brands} className={styles.textLink}>{t("cultureLink")}<ArrowUpRight size={14} /></Link>
          <figure className={styles.cultureTool}><Image src={props.culture.tool.url} alt={props.culture.tool.alt} fill quality={75} sizes="240px" className={styles.coverImage} style={{ objectPosition: props.culture.tool.position }} /></figure>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className={styles.brandsSection} aria-labelledby="brands-title">
        <header data-reveal="true"><p className={styles.eyebrow}>{t("brandsEyebrow")}</p><h2 id="brands-title">{t("brandsTitle")}</h2></header>
        <ul>
          {props.brands.map((brand, index) => (
            <li key={brand.id} data-reveal="true">
              <Link href={brand.href} className={styles.brandRow}>
                <span>{fmt(index + 1)}</span>
                <div>{brand.japaneseName ? <small>{brand.japaneseName}</small> : null}<strong>{brand.name}</strong></div>
                <em>{brand.origin}</em><ArrowUpRight size={14} />
              </Link>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </section>

      <section ref={ritualRef} className={styles.ritualStory} aria-labelledby="ritual-title">
        <div ref={ritualPinRef} className={styles.ritualPinned}>
          <header className={styles.ritualHeader}>
            <div><p className={styles.eyebrowLight}>{t("ritualEyebrow")}</p><h2 id="ritual-title">{t("ritualTitle")}</h2></div>
            <p>{t("ritualDescription")}</p>
          </header>
          <div className={styles.ritualStage}>
            <figure className={styles.ritualArtwork}>
              <Image src="/images/home/ritual/modern-matcha-ritual.webp" alt={t("ritualImageAlt")} fill quality={90} sizes="(max-width: 1100px) 100vw, 62vw" className={styles.ritualArtworkImage} />
            </figure>
            <ol className={styles.ritualSteps}>
              {props.ritual.map((step, index) => (
                <li key={step.title} data-ritual-step="true">
                  <span>{fmt(index + 1)}</span><div><h3>{step.title}</h3><p>{step.description}</p></div>
                  <figure><Image src={step.image.url} alt={step.image.alt} fill quality={75} sizes="112px" className={styles.coverImage} style={{ objectPosition: step.image.position }} /></figure>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ol>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section ref={journalRef} className={styles.journalSection} aria-labelledby="journal-title">
        <div className={styles.journalHeading} data-reveal="true">
          <div><p className={styles.eyebrow}>{t("journalEyebrow")}</p><h2 id="journal-title">{t("journalTitle")}</h2></div>
          <p>{t("journalDescription")}</p>
        </div>
        <article className={styles.journalFeature}>
          <div className={styles.journalFeatureCopy} data-reveal="true">
            <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{t("journalNote")}</p>
            <h3>{t("journalFeatureTitle")}</h3>
            <p>{t("journalFeatureDescription")}</p>
            <Link href={props.links.guide} className={styles.textLink}>{t("readGuide")}<ArrowUpRight size={14} /></Link>
          </div>
          <figure className={styles.journalFeatureMedia} data-journal-image="true">
            <Image src={props.journalHero.url} alt={props.journalHero.alt} fill quality={90} sizes="(max-width: 1100px) 100vw, 58vw" className={styles.journalHeroImage} style={{ objectPosition: props.journalHero.position }} />
          </figure>
        </article>
        <div ref={journalSliderRef} className={`keen-slider ${styles.journalSlider}`}>
          {props.journal.map((item, index) => (
            <article className={`keen-slider__slide ${styles.journalSlide}`} key={item.title}>
              <Link href={item.href}>
                <figure><Image src={item.image.url} alt={item.image.alt} fill quality={75} sizes="120px" className={styles.coverImage} style={{ objectPosition: item.image.position }} /></figure>
                <div><span>{fmt(index + 1)}</span><h3>{item.title}</h3><p>{item.description}</p></div><ArrowUpRight size={14} />
              </Link>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

File: .backup-before-rollback-v4.3-20260916-130342/components/home/interactive/SmoothScrollProvider.tsx
================
"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";
import Lenis from "lenis";
import "lenis/dist/lenis.css";
import gsap from "gsap";
import { ScrollTrigger } from "gsap/ScrollTrigger";

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

export default function SmoothScrollProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const reducedMotion = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
    if (reducedMotion) return;

    const lenis = new Lenis({
      lerp: 0.085,
      smoothWheel: true,
      syncTouch: false,
      wheelMultiplier: 0.9,
      touchMultiplier: 1.05,
    });

    const updateScrollTrigger = () => ScrollTrigger.update();
    const tick = (time: number) => lenis.raf(time * 1000);

    lenis.on("scroll", updateScrollTrigger);
    gsap.ticker.add(tick);
    gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0);

    const refreshId = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => ScrollTrigger.refresh());

    return () => {
      window.cancelAnimationFrame(refreshId);
      lenis.off("scroll", updateScrollTrigger);
      gsap.ticker.remove(tick);
      lenis.destroy();
    };
  }, []);

  return children;
}

File: .backup-before-rollback-v4.3-20260916-130342/i18n/request.ts

import { getRequestConfig } from "next-intl/server";

type SupportedLocale = "vi" | "en";

function normalizeLocale(value: string | undefined): SupportedLocale {
  return value?.toLowerCase().startsWith("en") ? "en" : "vi";
}

export default getRequestConfig(async ({ requestLocale }) => {
  const requestedLocale = await requestLocale;
  const locale = normalizeLocale(requestedLocale);

  return {
    locale,
    messages: {},
  };
});

      { title: "Storing matcha well", description: "Limit light, heat and air to help preserve aroma and flavor." },
      { title: "Understanding Usucha and Koicha", description: "Two whisked-tea styles with different ratios, textures and ways of revealing a profile." },
      { title: "Temperature and flavor", description: "Water temperature directly changes bitterness, softness and aromatic clarity." },
    ],
  },
};

const DISCOVERY_MEDIA_KEYS = ["tasteUmami", "tasteSweet", "tasteFloral", "tasteDaily"] as const satisfies readonly HomepageMediaKey[];
const RITUAL_MEDIA_KEYS = ["ritualSift", "ritualWater", "ritualWhisk", "ritualEnjoy"] as const satisfies readonly HomepageMediaKey[];
const SCORE_KEYS: ScoreKey[] = ["body_score", "umami_score", "sweetness_score", "bitterness_score"];

function scoreAverage(products: TeaProduct[], key: ScoreKey): number | null {
  const values = products
    .map((product) => product[key])
    .filter((value): value is number => typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value));
  if (!values.length) return null;
  return values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / values.length;
}

function getProductSummary(product: TeaProduct, vi: boolean): string {
  return (
    (vi ? product.tasting_summary_vi : product.tasting_summary_en) ||
    (vi ? product.description_vi : product.description_en) ||
    ""
  );
}

function formatPrice(product: TeaProduct, vi: boolean): string | null {
  if (typeof product.price !== "number") return null;
  const formatted = new Intl.NumberFormat(vi ? "vi-VN" : "en-US").format(product.price);
  return product.price_unit ? `${formatted}đ / ${product.price_unit}` : `${formatted}đ`;
}

function mediaAlt(slot: HomepageMediaSlot, vi: boolean) {
  return vi ? slot.altVi : slot.altEn;
}

function scorePosition(score: number) {
  return Math.min(100, Math.max(0, ((score - 1) / 4) * 100));
}

export async function HomePage() {
  const [{ lng }, media, brands, matcha] = await Promise.all([
    getT("common"),
    getHomepageMedia(),
    getTeaBrands(),
    getTeaProducts({ teaType: "matcha" }),
  ]);

  const vi = lng === "vi" || lng.startsWith("vi-");
  const c = COPY[vi ? "vi" : "en"];
  const featured = getHomepageFeaturedMatcha(matcha, 6);
  const scoreItems = featured.length ? featured : matcha.slice(0, 6);
  const visibleBrands = brands.filter((brand) => brand.is_visible).slice(0, 6);

  return (
    <main className={styles.root}>
      <div className={styles.folio}>
        <section className={styles.heroCollection} aria-labelledby="home-title">
          <article className={styles.heroPanel}>
            <Image
              src={media.heroMain.url}
              alt={mediaAlt(media.heroMain, vi)}
              fill
              priority
              quality={90}
              sizes="(max-width: 1050px) 100vw, 56vw"
              className={styles.coverImage}
              style={{ objectPosition: media.heroMain.position }}
            />
            <div className={styles.heroShade} aria-hidden="true" />
            <div className={styles.heroCopy}>
              <p className={styles.eyebrowLight}>{c.heroEyebrow}</p>
              <h1 id="home-title" lang={vi ? "vi" : "en"}>{c.heroTitle}</h1>
              <p className={styles.heroLead}>{c.heroDescription}</p>
              <Link href={withLocalePath("/matcha", lng)} className={styles.heroCta}>
                {c.explore}<ArrowUpRight aria-hidden="true" size={15} />
              </Link>
            </div>
            <div className={styles.heroVerticalNote} aria-hidden="true">一服の、やすらぎを。</div>
          </article>

          <aside className={styles.collectionPanel} aria-labelledby="collection-title">
            <header className={styles.collectionHeader}>
              <div>
                <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{c.collectionEyebrow}</p>
                <h2 id="collection-title">{c.collectionTitle}</h2>
              </div>
              <p>{c.collectionDescription}</p>
            </header>

            <ul className={styles.collectionGrid}>
              {featured.map((product) => {
                const productMedia = resolveProductMedia(product);
                const summary = getProductSummary(product, vi);
                const price = formatPrice(product, vi);
                const href = withLocalePath(`/matcha/${product.slug}`, lng);

                return (
                  <li key={product.id}>
                    <Link href={href} className={styles.productCard} aria-label={`${c.exploreProfile}: ${product.name}`}>
                      <figure className={styles.productMedia}>
                        {productMedia ? (
                          <Image
                            src={productMedia.src}
                            alt={vi ? productMedia.altVi : productMedia.altEn}
                            fill
                            quality={90}
                            sizes="(max-width: 720px) 44vw, (max-width: 1050px) 28vw, 220px"
                            className={productMedia.fit === "contain" ? styles.productImageContain : styles.productImageCover}
                            style={{ objectPosition: productMedia.position }}
                          />
                        ) : (
                          <span className={styles.productFallback}>{product.japanese_name || product.name}</span>
                        )}
                      </figure>
                      <div className={styles.productCopy}>
                        <p className={styles.productBrand}>{product.brand?.name || "Japanese Tea"}</p>
                        <h3>{product.name}</h3>
                        {summary ? <p className={styles.productSummary}>{summary}</p> : null}
                        <div className={styles.productFooter}>
                          {price ? <span className={styles.productPrice}>{price}</span> : <span>{product.origin_region || "Japan"}</span>}
                          <ArrowUpRight aria-hidden="true" size={13} />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </Link>
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
          </aside>
        </section>

        <section className={styles.originStrip} aria-labelledby="origin-title">
          <div className={styles.originCopy}>
            <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{c.storyEyebrow}</p>
            <h2 id="origin-title">{c.storyTitle}</h2>
            <p>{c.storyDescription}</p>
            <Link href={withLocalePath("/brands", lng)} className={styles.textLink}>
              {c.discoverOrigin}<ArrowUpRight aria-hidden="true" size={14} />
            </Link>
          </div>
          <div className={styles.originMosaic}>
            <figure className={styles.originLarge}>
              <Image src={media.storyLandscape.url} alt={mediaAlt(media.storyLandscape, vi)} fill quality={90} sizes="(max-width: 1050px) 100vw, 520px" className={styles.coverImage} style={{ objectPosition: media.storyLandscape.position }} />
            </figure>
            <figure>
              <Image src={media.spectrumBowl.url} alt={mediaAlt(media.spectrumBowl, vi)} fill quality={90} sizes="260px" className={styles.coverImage} style={{ objectPosition: media.spectrumBowl.position }} />
            </figure>
            <figure>
              <Image src={media.ritualPoster.url} alt={mediaAlt(media.ritualPoster, vi)} fill quality={90} sizes="260px" className={styles.coverImage} style={{ objectPosition: media.ritualPoster.position }} />
            </figure>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className={styles.tasteSpectrum} aria-labelledby="taste-title">
          <div className={styles.tasteIntro}>
            <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{c.discoveryEyebrow}</p>
            <h2 id="taste-title">{c.discoveryTitle}</h2>
            <p>{c.discoveryDescription}</p>
          </div>

          <ul className={styles.tasteList}>
            {c.discoveryCards.map((card, index) => {
              const slot = media[DISCOVERY_MEDIA_KEYS[index]];
              return (
                <li key={card.title}>
                  <Link href={withLocalePath("/matcha", lng)} className={styles.tasteCard}>
                    <figure>
                      <Image src={slot.url} alt={mediaAlt(slot, vi)} fill quality={90} sizes="(max-width: 720px) 42vw, 240px" className={styles.coverImage} style={{ objectPosition: slot.position }} />
                    </figure>
                    <div>
                      <h3>{card.title}</h3>
                      <p>{card.description}</p>
                    </div>
                    <ArrowUpRight aria-hidden="true" size={14} />
                  </Link>
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>

          <figure className={styles.spectrumPlate} aria-labelledby="spectrum-title">
            <figcaption>
              <span className={styles.spectrumKanji}>味</span>
              <div>
                <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{vi ? "ĐỌC PROFILE" : "READ THE PROFILE"}</p>
                <h2 id="spectrum-title">{c.spectrumTitle}</h2>
              </div>
            </figcaption>
            <dl className={styles.axisList}>
              {SCORE_KEYS.map((key) => {
                const score = scoreAverage(scoreItems, key);
                const labels = c.scoreLabels[key];
                const position = score === null ? 50 : scorePosition(score);
                return (
                  <div className={styles.axisRow} key={key}>
                    <dt>
                      <strong>{labels.title}</strong>
                      <span>{labels.low} — {labels.high}</span>
                    </dt>
                    <dd>
                      <div
                        className={`${styles.axisTrack} ${score === null ? styles.axisTrackEmpty : ""}`}
                        style={score === null ? undefined : ({ "--axis-x": `${position}%` } as CSSProperties)}
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      >
                        <i className={styles.axisStart} />
                        <i className={styles.axisMid} />
                        <i className={styles.axisEnd} />
                        {score === null ? (
                          <span className={styles.axisMissing} />
                        ) : (
                          <span className={styles.axisDot} />
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <div className={styles.axisLabels}><span>{labels.low}</span><span>{labels.high}</span></div>
                      <span className={styles.axisValue}>{score === null ? c.spectrumEmpty : `${score.toFixed(1)} / 5`}</span>
                    </dd>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </dl>
          </figure>
        </section>

        <section className={styles.brandSection} aria-labelledby="brands-title">
          <header>
            <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{vi ? "TEA HOUSES" : "TEA HOUSES"}</p>
            <h2 id="brands-title">{c.brandsTitle}</h2>
          </header>
          <ul className={styles.brandList}>
            {visibleBrands.map((brand) => (
              <li key={brand.id}>
                <Link href={withLocalePath(`/brands/${brand.slug}`, lng)} className={styles.brandRow}>
                  <div>
                    {brand.japanese_name ? <span>{brand.japanese_name}</span> : null}
                    <strong>{brand.name}</strong>
                  </div>
                  <span>{brand.origin_region || brand.origin_country || "Japan"}</span>
                  <span className={styles.brandAction}>{c.viewBrand}<ArrowUpRight aria-hidden="true" size={13} /></span>
                </Link>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </section>
        <section className={styles.modernRitualSection} aria-labelledby="ritual-title">
          <header className={styles.modernRitualHeader}>
            <div>
              <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{c.ritualEyebrow}</p>
              <h2 id="ritual-title">{c.ritualTitle}</h2>

                <p>{vi ? "Chậm lại một nhịp để nhận ra độ ngọt, umami, hương và hậu vị trong từng chén trà." : "Slow the pace and notice sweetness, umami, aroma and finish in every bowl."}</p>
                <Link href={withLocalePath("/guide", lng)} className={styles.textLink}>

            </div>

            <figure className={styles.journalHeroMedia}>
              <Image
                src="/images/home/journal/journal-mindful-whisk.webp"

                fill
                quality={90}

                className={styles.journalHeroImage}
              />

                <span>MIE MATCHA</span>
                <span>{vi ? "Một chén trà tĩnh hơn" : "A quieter cup"}</span>
              </figcaption>
            </figure>
          </div>

          <ul className={styles.journalReadingGrid} aria-label={vi ? "Ghi chú hướng dẫn về matcha" : "Matcha guide notes"}>
            {c.journalCards.map((card, index) => {
              const slot = [media.spectrumBowl, media.heroOrigin, media.storyBowl, media.tasteDaily][index];
              return (
                <li key={card.title}>

                      <h3>{card.title}</h3>

                  </Link>
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

      { title: "Add water", description: "Pour 60–80 ml of water at around 70–80°C for a balanced extraction." },
      { title: "Whisk", description: "Whisk in a quick zigzag motion until smooth with a light foam." },
      { title: "Taste it pure", description: "Notice the opening aroma, body and finish before adding anything else." },
      { title: "Make it yours", description: "Add ice or milk when you want a more modern matcha drink." },
    ],
    journalEyebrow: "TEA JOURNAL",
    journalTitle: "Guidance for a more mindful bowl.",
    journalDescription: "Concise notes on flavor, brewing and storage to make every bowl easier to understand and enjoy.",
    journalFeatureTitle: "Listen to the tea and notice each flavor note at a slower pace.",
    readGuide: "Read the guide",
    journalCards: [
      { title: "Understanding umami", description: "Notice natural sweetness, roundness and depth instead of simply calling it strong." },
      { title: "Storing matcha well", description: "Limit light, heat and air to help preserve aroma and flavor." },
      { title: "Understanding Usucha and Koicha", description: "Two whisked-tea styles with different ratios, textures and ways of revealing a profile." },
      { title: "Temperature and flavor", description: "Water temperature directly changes bitterness, softness and aromatic clarity." },
    ],
  },
};

const DISCOVERY_MEDIA_KEYS = ["tasteUmami", "tasteSweet", "tasteFloral", "tasteDaily"] as const satisfies readonly HomepageMediaKey[];
const RITUAL_MEDIA_KEYS = ["ritualSift", "ritualWater", "ritualWhisk", "ritualEnjoy"] as const satisfies readonly HomepageMediaKey[];
const SCORE_KEYS: ScoreKey[] = ["body_score", "umami_score", "sweetness_score", "bitterness_score"];

function scoreAverage(products: TeaProduct[], key: ScoreKey): number | null {
  const values = products
    .map((product) => product[key])
    .filter((value): value is number => typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value));
  if (!values.length) return null;
  return values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / values.length;
}

function getProductSummary(product: TeaProduct, vi: boolean): string {
  return (
    (vi ? product.tasting_summary_vi : product.tasting_summary_en) ||
    (vi ? product.description_vi : product.description_en) ||
    ""
  );
}

function formatPrice(product: TeaProduct, vi: boolean): string | null {
  if (typeof product.price !== "number") return null;
  const formatted = new Intl.NumberFormat(vi ? "vi-VN" : "en-US").format(product.price);
  return product.price_unit ? `${formatted}đ / ${product.price_unit}` : `${formatted}đ`;
}

function mediaAlt(slot: HomepageMediaSlot, vi: boolean) {
  return vi ? slot.altVi : slot.altEn;
}

function scorePosition(score: number) {
  return Math.min(100, Math.max(0, ((score - 1) / 4) * 100));
}

export async function HomePage() {
  const [{ lng }, media, brands, matcha] = await Promise.all([
    getT("common"),
    getHomepageMedia(),
    getTeaBrands(),
    getTeaProducts({ teaType: "matcha" }),
  ]);

  const vi = lng === "vi" || lng.startsWith("vi-");
  const c = COPY[vi ? "vi" : "en"];
  const featured = getHomepageFeaturedMatcha(matcha, 6);
  const scoreItems = featured.length ? featured : matcha.slice(0, 6);
  const visibleBrands = brands.filter((brand) => brand.is_visible).slice(0, 6);

  return (
    <main className={styles.root}>
      <div className={styles.folio}>
        <section className={styles.heroCollection} aria-labelledby="home-title">
          <article className={styles.heroPanel}>
            <Image
              src={media.heroMain.url}
              alt={mediaAlt(media.heroMain, vi)}
              fill
              priority
              quality={90}
              sizes="(max-width: 1050px) 100vw, 56vw"
              className={styles.coverImage}
              style={{ objectPosition: media.heroMain.position }}
            />
            <div className={styles.heroShade} aria-hidden="true" />
            <div className={styles.heroCopy}>
              <p className={styles.eyebrowLight}>{c.heroEyebrow}</p>
              <h1 id="home-title" lang={vi ? "vi" : "en"}>{c.heroTitle}</h1>
              <p className={styles.heroLead}>{c.heroDescription}</p>
              <Link href={withLocalePath("/matcha", lng)} className={styles.heroCta}>
                {c.explore}<ArrowUpRight aria-hidden="true" size={15} />
              </Link>
            </div>
            <div className={styles.heroVerticalNote} aria-hidden="true">一服の、やすらぎを。</div>
          </article>

          <aside className={styles.collectionPanel} aria-labelledby="collection-title">
            <header className={styles.collectionHeader}>
              <div>
                <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{c.collectionEyebrow}</p>
                <h2 id="collection-title">{c.collectionTitle}</h2>
              </div>
              <p>{c.collectionDescription}</p>
            </header>

            <ul className={styles.collectionGrid}>
              {featured.map((product) => {
                const productMedia = resolveProductMedia(product);
                const summary = getProductSummary(product, vi);
                const price = formatPrice(product, vi);
                const href = withLocalePath(`/matcha/${product.slug}`, lng);

                return (
                  <li key={product.id}>
                    <Link href={href} className={styles.productCard} aria-label={`${c.exploreProfile}: ${product.name}`}>
                      <figure className={styles.productMedia}>
                        {productMedia ? (
                          <Image
                            src={productMedia.src}
                            alt={vi ? productMedia.altVi : productMedia.altEn}
                            fill
                            quality={90}
                            sizes="(max-width: 720px) 44vw, (max-width: 1050px) 28vw, 220px"
                            className={productMedia.fit === "contain" ? styles.productImageContain : styles.productImageCover}
                            style={{ objectPosition: productMedia.position }}
                          />
                        ) : (
                          <span className={styles.productFallback}>{product.japanese_name || product.name}</span>
                        )}
                      </figure>
                      <div className={styles.productCopy}>
                        <p className={styles.productBrand}>{product.brand?.name || "Japanese Tea"}</p>
                        <h3>{product.name}</h3>
                        {summary ? <p className={styles.productSummary}>{summary}</p> : null}
                        <div className={styles.productFooter}>
                          {price ? <span className={styles.productPrice}>{price}</span> : <span>{product.origin_region || "Japan"}</span>}
                          <ArrowUpRight aria-hidden="true" size={13} />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </Link>
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
          </aside>
        </section>

        <section className={styles.originStrip} aria-labelledby="origin-title">
          <div className={styles.originCopy}>
            <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{c.storyEyebrow}</p>
            <h2 id="origin-title">{c.storyTitle}</h2>
            <p>{c.storyDescription}</p>
            <Link href={withLocalePath("/brands", lng)} className={styles.textLink}>
              {c.discoverOrigin}<ArrowUpRight aria-hidden="true" size={14} />

                    <div>
                      <h3>{card.title}</h3>
                      <p>{card.description}</p>
                    </div>
                    <ArrowUpRight aria-hidden="true" size={14} />

            })}
          </ul>

          <figure className={styles.spectrumPlate} aria-labelledby="spectrum-title">
            <figcaption>
              <span className={styles.spectrumKanji}>味</span>
              <div>
                <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{vi ? "ĐỌC PROFILE" : "READ THE PROFILE"}</p>
                <h2 id="spectrum-title">{c.spectrumTitle}</h2>
              </div>
            </figcaption>
            <dl className={styles.axisList}>
              {SCORE_KEYS.map((key) => {
                const score = scoreAverage(scoreItems, key);
                const labels = c.scoreLabels[key];
                const position = score === null ? 50 : scorePosition(score);
                return (
                  <div className={styles.axisRow} key={key}>
                    <dt>
                      <strong>{labels.title}</strong>
                      <span>{labels.low} — {labels.high}</span>
                    </dt>
                    <dd>
                      <div
                        className={`${styles.axisTrack} ${score === null ? styles.axisTrackEmpty : ""}`}
                        style={score === null ? undefined : ({ "--axis-x": `${position}%` } as CSSProperties)}
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      >
                        <i className={styles.axisStart} />
                        <i className={styles.axisMid} />
                        <i className={styles.axisEnd} />
                        {score === null ? (
                          <span className={styles.axisMissing} />
                        ) : (
                          <span className={styles.axisDot} />
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <div className={styles.axisLabels}><span>{labels.low}</span><span>{labels.high}</span></div>
                      <span className={styles.axisValue}>{score === null ? c.spectrumEmpty : `${score.toFixed(1)} / 5`}</span>
                    </dd>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </dl>
          </figure>
        </section>

        <section className={styles.brandSection} aria-labelledby="brands-title">
          <header>
            <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{vi ? "TEA HOUSES" : "TEA HOUSES"}</p>

          </header>
          <ul className={styles.brandList}>

                    <strong>{brand.name}</strong>

      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

      umami_score: { title: "Umami", low: "Low", high: "High" },
      sweetness_score: { title: "Sweetness", low: "Soft", high: "Clear" },
      bitterness_score: { title: "Bitterness", low: "Gentle", high: "Bold" },
    },
    storyEyebrow: "TEA CULTURE",
    storyTitle: "From growing place to a bowl with context.",
    storyDescription:
      "Origin, tea houses, tools and brewing practice create the context that makes each profile easier to understand.",
    brandsTitle: "Tea houses in the archive",
    viewBrand: "View tea house",
    ritualEyebrow: "BREWING RITUAL",
    ritualTitle: "Craftsmanship — five steps, one rhythm.",
    ritualDescription: "A modern, repeatable ritual: measure well, use the right temperature, whisk with purpose, taste first, then make it yours.",
    ritualSteps: [
      { title: "Measure matcha", description: "Use about 1–2 g of matcha for one bowl or glass." },
      { title: "Add water", description: "Pour 60–80 ml of water at around 70–80°C for a balanced extraction." },
      { title: "Whisk", description: "Whisk in a quick zigzag motion until smooth with a light foam." },
      { title: "Taste it pure", description: "Notice the opening aroma, body and finish before adding anything else." },
      { title: "Make it yours", description: "Add ice or milk when you want a more modern matcha drink." },
    ],
    journalEyebrow: "TEA JOURNAL",
    journalTitle: "Guidance for a more mindful bowl.",
    journalDescription: "Concise notes on flavor, brewing and storage to make every bowl easier to understand and enjoy.",
    journalFeatureTitle: "Listen to the tea and notice each flavor note at a slower pace.",
    readGuide: "Read the guide",
    journalCards: [
      { title: "Understanding umami", description: "Notice natural sweetness, roundness and depth instead of simply calling it strong." },
      { title: "Storing matcha well", description: "Limit light, heat and air to help preserve aroma and flavor." },
      { title: "Understanding Usucha and Koicha", description: "Two whisked-tea styles with different ratios, textures and ways of revealing a profile." },
      { title: "Temperature and flavor", description: "Water temperature directly changes bitterness, softness and aromatic clarity." },
    ],
  },
};

const DISCOVERY_MEDIA_KEYS = ["tasteUmami", "tasteSweet", "tasteFloral", "tasteDaily"] as const satisfies readonly HomepageMediaKey[];
const RITUAL_MEDIA_KEYS = ["ritualSift", "ritualWater", "ritualWhisk", "ritualEnjoy"] as const satisfies readonly HomepageMediaKey[];
const SCORE_KEYS: ScoreKey[] = ["body_score", "umami_score", "sweetness_score", "bitterness_score"];

function scoreAverage(products: TeaProduct[], key: ScoreKey): number | null {
  const values = products
    .map((product) => product[key])
    .filter((value): value is number => typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value));
  if (!values.length) return null;
  return values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / values.length;
}

function getProductSummary(product: TeaProduct, vi: boolean): string {
  return (
    (vi ? product.tasting_summary_vi : product.tasting_summary_en) ||
    (vi ? product.description_vi : product.description_en) ||
    ""
  );
}

function formatPrice(product: TeaProduct, vi: boolean): string | null {
  if (typeof product.price !== "number") return null;
  const formatted = new Intl.NumberFormat(vi ? "vi-VN" : "en-US").format(product.price);
  return product.price_unit ? `${formatted}đ / ${product.price_unit}` : `${formatted}đ`;
}

function mediaAlt(slot: HomepageMediaSlot, vi: boolean) {
  return vi ? slot.altVi : slot.altEn;
}

function scorePosition(score: number) {
  return Math.min(100, Math.max(0, ((score - 1) / 4) * 100));
}

export async function HomePage() {
  const [{ lng }, media, brands, matcha] = await Promise.all([
    getT("common"),
    getHomepageMedia(),
    getTeaBrands(),
    getTeaProducts({ teaType: "matcha" }),
  ]);

  const vi = lng === "vi" || lng.startsWith("vi-");
  const c = COPY[vi ? "vi" : "en"];
  const featured = getHomepageFeaturedMatcha(matcha, 6);
  const scoreItems = featured.length ? featured : matcha.slice(0, 6);
  const visibleBrands = brands.filter((brand) => brand.is_visible).slice(0, 6);

  return (
    <main className={styles.root}>
      <div className={styles.folio}>
        <section className={styles.heroCollection} aria-labelledby="home-title">

              alt={mediaAlt(media.heroMain, vi)}
              fill
              priority
              quality={90}

              className={styles.coverImage}
              style={{ objectPosition: media.heroMain.position }}
            />
            <div className={styles.heroShade} aria-hidden="true" />
            <div className={styles.heroCopy}>
              <p className={styles.eyebrowLight}>{c.heroEyebrow}</p>
              <h1 id="home-title" lang={vi ? "vi" : "en"}>{c.heroTitle}</h1>
              <p className={styles.heroLead}>{c.heroDescription}</p>
              <Link href={withLocalePath("/matcha", lng)} className={styles.heroCta}>
                {c.explore}<ArrowUpRight aria-hidden="true" size={15} />
              </Link>
            </div>
            <div className={styles.heroVerticalNote} aria-hidden="true">一服の、やすらぎを。</div>
          </article>

          <aside className={styles.collectionPanel} aria-labelledby="collection-title">
            <header className={styles.collectionHeader}>
              <div>
                <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{c.collectionEyebrow}</p>
                <h2 id="collection-title">{c.collectionTitle}</h2>
              </div>
              <p>{c.collectionDescription}</p>
            </header>

            <ul className={styles.collectionGrid}>
              {featured.map((product) => {
                const productMedia = resolveProductMedia(product);
                const summary = getProductSummary(product, vi);
                const price = formatPrice(product, vi);
                const href = withLocalePath(`/matcha/${product.slug}`, lng);

                return (
                  <li key={product.id}>
                    <Link href={href} className={styles.productCard} aria-label={`${c.exploreProfile}: ${product.name}`}>
                      <figure className={styles.productMedia}>
                        {productMedia ? (
                          <Image
                            src={productMedia.src}
                            alt={vi ? productMedia.altVi : productMedia.altEn}
                            fill
                            quality={90}
                            sizes="(max-width: 720px) 44vw, (max-width: 1050px) 28vw, 220px"
                            className={productMedia.fit === "contain" ? styles.productImageContain : styles.productImageCover}
                            style={{ objectPosition: productMedia.position }}
                          />
                        ) : (
                          <span className={styles.productFallback}>{product.japanese_name || product.name}</span>
                        )}
                      </figure>
                      <div className={styles.productCopy}>
                        <p className={styles.productBrand}>{product.brand?.name || "Japanese Tea"}</p>
                        <h3>{product.name}</h3>
                        {summary ? <p className={styles.productSummary}>{summary}</p> : null}
                        <div className={styles.productFooter}>
                          {price ? <span className={styles.productPrice}>{price}</span> : <span>{product.origin_region || "Japan"}</span>}
                          <ArrowUpRight aria-hidden="true" size={13} />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </Link>
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
          </aside>
        </section>

        <section className={styles.originStrip} aria-labelledby="origin-title">
          <div className={styles.originCopy}>
            <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{c.storyEyebrow}</p>

            <p>{c.storyDescription}</p>

                const position = score === null ? 50 : scorePosition(score);
                return (
                  <div className={styles.axisRow} key={key}>
                    <dt>
                      <strong>{labels.title}</strong>
                      <span>{labels.low} — {labels.high}</span>
                    </dt>
                    <dd>
                      <div
                        className={`${styles.axisTrack} ${score === null ? styles.axisTrackEmpty : ""}`}
                        style={score === null ? undefined : ({ "--axis-x": `${position}%` } as CSSProperties)}
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      >
                        <i className={styles.axisStart} />
                        <i className={styles.axisMid} />
                        <i className={styles.axisEnd} />
                        {score === null ? (
                          <span className={styles.axisMissing} />
                        ) : (
                          <span className={styles.axisDot} />
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <div className={styles.axisLabels}><span>{labels.low}</span><span>{labels.high}</span></div>
                      <span className={styles.axisValue}>{score === null ? c.spectrumEmpty : `${score.toFixed(1)} / 5`}</span>
                    </dd>
                  </div>

File: .backup-hero-collection-carousel-v4.5.3-20260916072025/components/home/interactive/FeaturedShowcase.tsx
================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, ArrowUpRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useKeenSlider } from "keen-slider/react";
import { withLocalePath } from "@/lib/i18n/routing";
import styles from "./FeaturedShowcase.module.css";

type SlideItem = {
  src: string;
  alt: string;
  eyebrow: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
};

type ProductItem = {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  name: string;
  japaneseName?: string;
  brandName: string;
  summary: string;
  origin: string;
  price?: string | null;
  image?: {
    src: string;
    alt: string;
    position?: string;
    fit?: "contain" | "cover";
  };
};

type Props = {
  locale: string;
  eyebrow: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  ctaLabel: string;
  exploreLabel: string;
  referencePriceLabel: string;
  autoSwitchLabel: string;
  dragLabel: string;
  slides: SlideItem[];
  products: ProductItem[];
};

export function FeaturedShowcase({
  locale,
  eyebrow,
  title,
  description,
  ctaLabel,
  exploreLabel,
  referencePriceLabel,
  autoSwitchLabel,
  dragLabel,
  slides,
  products,
}: Props) {
  const [heroIndex, setHeroIndex] = useState(0);
  const [productIndex, setProductIndex] = useState(0);
  const [productLoaded, setProductLoaded] = useState(false);

  const autoplay = useMemo(
    () => (slider: any) => {
      let timeout: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;
      let mouseOver = false;

      const clearNextTimeout = () => clearTimeout(timeout);
      const nextTimeout = () => {
        clearNextTimeout();
        if (mouseOver) return;
        timeout = setTimeout(() => {
          slider.next();
        }, 4200);
      };

      slider.on("created", () => {
        slider.container.addEventListener("mouseenter", () => {
          mouseOver = true;
          clearNextTimeout();
        });
        slider.container.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => {
          mouseOver = false;
          nextTimeout();
        });
        nextTimeout();
      });

      slider.on("dragStarted", clearNextTimeout);
      slider.on("animationEnded", nextTimeout);
      slider.on("updated", nextTimeout);
    },
    []
  );

  const [heroRef, heroSlider] = useKeenSlider(
    {
      loop: true,
      rubberband: false,
      slides: { perView: 1, spacing: 0 },
      created(slider) {
        setHeroIndex(slider.track.details.rel);
      },
      slideChanged(slider) {
        setHeroIndex(slider.track.details.rel);
      },
    },
    [autoplay]
  );

  const [productRef, productSlider] = useKeenSlider(
    {
      loop: false,
      rubberband: true,
      slides: { perView: 1.06, spacing: 16 },
      breakpoints: {
        "(min-width: 720px)": {
          slides: { perView: 1.18, spacing: 18 },
        },
        "(min-width: 1024px)": {
          slides: { perView: 1.28, spacing: 20 },
        },
        "(min-width: 1360px)": {
          slides: { perView: 2.02, spacing: 22 },
        },
      },
      created(slider) {
        setProductLoaded(true);
        setProductIndex(slider.track.details.rel);
      },
      slideChanged(slider) {
        setProductIndex(slider.track.details.rel);
      },
    }
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      heroSlider.current?.destroy();
      productSlider.current?.destroy();
    };
  }, [heroSlider, productSlider]);

  const totalProducts = products.length;
  const isAtStart = productIndex <= 0;
  const isAtEnd =
    productSlider.current?.track?.details
      ? productIndex >= productSlider.current.track.details.maxIdx
      : false;

  return (
    <section className={styles.section} aria-labelledby="featured-showcase-title">
      <div className={styles.shell}>
        <div className={styles.layout}>
          <div className={styles.mediaPane}>
            <div ref={heroRef} className={`keen-slider ${styles.heroSlider}`}>
              {slides.map((slide, index) => (
                <div key={`${slide.title}-${index}`} className={`keen-slider__slide ${styles.heroSlide}`}>
                  <div className={styles.heroMedia}>
                    <Image
                      src={slide.src}
                      alt={slide.alt}
                      fill
                      priority={index === 0}
                      quality={92}
                      sizes="(max-width: 1023px) 100vw, 52vw"
                      className={styles.heroImage}
                    />
                    <div className={styles.heroOverlay} />
                  </div>

                  <div className={styles.heroCopy}>
                    <p className={styles.heroEyebrow}>{slide.eyebrow}</p>
                    <h2 className={styles.heroTitle}>{slide.title}</h2>
                    <p className={styles.heroDescription}>{slide.description}</p>

                    <div className={styles.heroActions}>
                      <Link href={withLocalePath("/matcha", locale)} className={styles.heroCta}>
                        {ctaLabel}
                        <ArrowUpRight size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
                      </Link>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className={styles.heroFooter}>
              <div className={styles.heroDots}>
                {slides.map((_, idx) => (
                  <button
                    key={idx}
                    type="button"
                    aria-label={`Go to slide ${idx + 1}`}
                    className={`${styles.heroDot} ${heroIndex === idx ? styles.heroDotActive : ""}`}
                    onClick={() => heroSlider.current?.moveToIdx(idx)}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>

              <div className={styles.heroMeta}>{autoSwitchLabel}</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className={styles.productPane}>
            <div className={styles.header}>
              <div className={styles.headerCopy}>
                <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{eyebrow}</p>
                <h2 id="featured-showcase-title" className={styles.title}>
                  {title}
                </h2>
              </div>

              <div className={styles.headerAside}>
                <p className={styles.description}>{description}</p>
                <div className={styles.toolbar}>
                  <span className={styles.dragHint}>{dragLabel}</span>

                  <div className={styles.controls}>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className={styles.navButton}
                      onClick={() => productSlider.current?.prev()}
                      disabled={!productLoaded || isAtStart}
                      aria-label="Previous products"
                    >
                      <ArrowLeft size={16} />
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className={styles.navButton}
                      onClick={() => productSlider.current?.next()}
                      disabled={!productLoaded || isAtEnd}
                      aria-label="Next products"
                    >
                      <ArrowRight size={16} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div ref={productRef} className={`keen-slider ${styles.productSlider}`}>
              {products.map((product) => (
                <div key={product.id} className={`keen-slider__slide ${styles.productSlide}`}>
                  <Link
                    href={withLocalePath(`/matcha/${product.slug}`, locale)}
                    className={styles.productCard}
                    aria-label={`${exploreLabel}: ${product.name}`}
                  >
                    <figure className={styles.productMedia}>
                      <div className={styles.productMediaInner}>
                        {product.image ? (
                          <Image
                            src={product.image.src}
                            alt={product.image.alt}
                            fill
                            quality={92}
                            sizes="(max-width: 1023px) 82vw, (max-width: 1359px) 36vw, 24vw"
                            className={
                              product.image.fit === "contain"
                                ? styles.productImageContain
                                : styles.productImageCover
                            }
                            style={{ objectPosition: product.image.position || "50% 50%" }}
                          />
                        ) : (
                          <div className={styles.productFallback}>
                            <span>{product.japaneseName || product.name}</span>
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </figure>

                    <div className={styles.productBody}>
                      <p className={styles.productBrand}>{product.brandName}</p>

                      <div className={styles.productHeading}>
                        <div>
                          {product.japaneseName ? (
                            <span className={styles.productJapanese}>{product.japaneseName}</span>
                          ) : null}
                          <h3>{product.name}</h3>
                        </div>
                        <ArrowUpRight size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
                      </div>

                      <p className={styles.productSummary}>{product.summary}</p>

                      <div className={styles.productMeta}>
                        <span>{product.origin}</span>
                        {product.price ? (
                          <span className={styles.productPrice}>
                            {referencePriceLabel}: {product.price}
                          </span>
                        ) : null}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </Link>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            {totalProducts > 1 ? (
              <div className={styles.counter}>
                <span>
                  {String(productIndex + 1).padStart(2, "0")} / {String(totalProducts).padStart(2, "0")}
                </span>
              </div>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

const DISCOVERY_MEDIA_KEYS = ["tasteUmami", "tasteSweet", "tasteFloral", "tasteDaily"] as const satisfies readonly HomepageMediaKey[];
const RITUAL_MEDIA_KEYS = ["ritualSift", "ritualWater", "ritualWhisk", "ritualEnjoy", "tasteDaily"] as const satisfies readonly HomepageMediaKey[];
const JOURNAL_MEDIA_KEYS = ["spectrumBowl", "heroOrigin", "storyBowl", "tasteDaily"] as const satisfies readonly HomepageMediaKey[];
const SCORE_KEYS: ScoreKey[] = ["body_score", "umami_score", "sweetness_score", "bitterness_score"];

function scoreAverage(products: TeaProduct[], key: ScoreKey): number | null {
  const values = products.map((product) => product[key]).filter((value): value is number => typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value));
  return values.length ? values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / values.length : null;
}

function getSummary(product: TeaProduct, vi: boolean) {
  return (vi ? product.tasting_summary_vi : product.tasting_summary_en) || (vi ? product.description_vi : product.description_en) || "";
}

function formatPrice(product: TeaProduct, vi: boolean): string | null {
  if (typeof product.price !== "number") return null;
  const formatted = new Intl.NumberFormat(vi ? "vi-VN" : "en-US").format(product.price);
  return product.price_unit ? `${formatted}đ / ${product.price_unit}` : `${formatted}đ`;
}

function toMedia(slot: HomepageMediaSlot, vi: boolean): MediaItem {
  return { url: slot.url, alt: vi ? slot.altVi : slot.altEn, position: slot.position };
}

function scorePosition(score: number) {
  return Math.min(100, Math.max(0, ((score - 1) / 4) * 100));
}

function scalarMessages(copy: Copy) {
  const { discoveryCards: _a, scoreLabels: _b, ritualSteps: _c, journalCards: _d, ...messages } = copy;
  return messages;
}

export async function HomePage() {
  const [{ lng }, media, brands, matcha] = await Promise.all([
    getT("common"),
    getHomepageMedia(),
    getTeaBrands(),
    getTeaProducts({ teaType: "matcha" }),
  ]);

  const locale: LocaleCode = lng === "vi" || lng.startsWith("vi-") ? "vi" : "en";
  const vi = locale === "vi";
  const copy = COPY[locale];
  const featured = getHomepageFeaturedMatcha(matcha, 6);
  const scoreItems = featured.length ? featured : matcha.slice(0, 6);
  const visibleBrands = brands.filter((brand) => brand.is_visible).slice(0, 6);

  const products: HomeExperienceProps["products"] = featured.map((product) => {
    const image = resolveProductMedia(product);
    return {
      id: product.id,
      name: product.name,
      japaneseName: product.japanese_name || null,
      brand: product.brand?.name || "Japanese Tea",
      summary: getSummary(product, vi),
      price: formatPrice(product, vi),
      origin: product.origin_region || product.origin_country || "Japan",
      href: withLocalePath(`/matcha/${product.slug}`, lng),
      image: image ? { src: image.src, alt: vi ? image.altVi : image.altEn, fit: image.fit, position: image.position } : null,
    };
  });

  const tasteItems: HomeExperienceProps["tasteItems"] = copy.discoveryCards.map((card, index) => ({
    ...card,
    href: withLocalePath("/matcha", lng),
    image: toMedia(media[DISCOVERY_MEDIA_KEYS[index]], vi),
  }));

  const spectrum: HomeExperienceProps["spectrum"] = SCORE_KEYS.map((key) => {
    const score = scoreAverage(scoreItems, key);
    const labels = copy.scoreLabels[key];
    return { key, ...labels, score, position: score === null ? 50 : scorePosition(score) };
  });

  const ritual: HomeExperienceProps["ritual"] = copy.ritualSteps.map((step, index) => ({
    ...step,
    image: toMedia(media[RITUAL_MEDIA_KEYS[index]], vi),
  }));

  const journal: HomeExperienceProps["journal"] = copy.journalCards.map((card, index) => ({
    ...card,
    href: withLocalePath("/guide", lng),
    image: toMedia(media[JOURNAL_MEDIA_KEYS[index]], vi),
  }));

  const props: HomeExperienceProps = {
    locale,
    links: {
      matcha: withLocalePath("/matcha", lng),
      brands: withLocalePath("/brands", lng),
      guide: withLocalePath("/guide", lng),
    },
    hero: { image: toMedia(media.heroMain, vi), detail: toMedia(media.heroOrigin, vi) },
    products,
    tasteItems,
    spectrum,
    brands: visibleBrands.map((brand) => ({ id: brand.id, name: brand.name, japaneseName: brand.japanese_name || null, origin: brand.origin_region || brand.origin_country || "Japan", href: withLocalePath(`/brands/${brand.slug}`, lng) })),
    culture: { landscape: toMedia(media.storyLandscape, vi), detail: toMedia(media.spectrumBowl, vi), tool: toMedia(media.ritualPoster, vi) },
    ritual,
    journal,
    journalHero: toMedia(media.journalFeature, vi),
  };

  return (
    <main className={styles.root}>
      <NextIntlClientProvider locale={locale} messages={{ Home: scalarMessages(copy) }}>
        <SmoothScrollProvider>
          <HomeExperience {...props} />
        </SmoothScrollProvider>
      </NextIntlClientProvider>
    </main>
  );
}

File: app/[lng]/(site)/about/page.tsx
