import { optimizeCloudinaryUrl } from "@/lib/cloudinary/url";
import type { TeaProduct } from "@/lib/types/tea";

export type ProductMedia = {
  src: string;
  fit: "contain" | "cover";
  position: string;
  altVi: string;
  altEn: string;
  source: "curated" | "product";
};

const curatedProductMedia: Record<string, ProductMedia> = {
  isuzu: {
    src: "/images/tea/marukyu/isuzu.webp",
    fit: "contain",
    position: "center",
    altVi: "Hộp Matcha Isuzu của Marukyu Koyamaen",
    altEn: "Marukyu Koyamaen Isuzu matcha tin",
    source: "curated",
  },
  "chigi-no-shiro": {
    src: "/images/tea/marukyu/chigi-no-shiro.webp",
    fit: "contain",
    position: "center",
    altVi: "Hộp Matcha Chigi no Shiro của Marukyu Koyamaen",
    altEn: "Marukyu Koyamaen Chigi no Shiro matcha tin",
    source: "curated",
  },
  yugen: {
    src: "/images/tea/marukyu/yugen.webp",
    fit: "contain",
    position: "center",
    altVi: "Hộp Matcha Yugen của Marukyu Koyamaen",
    altEn: "Marukyu Koyamaen Yugen matcha tin",
    source: "curated",
  },
  "wako-marukyu-koyamaen": {
    src: "/images/tea/marukyu/wako.webp",
    fit: "contain",
    position: "center",
    altVi: "Hộp Matcha Wako của Marukyu Koyamaen",
    altEn: "Marukyu Koyamaen Wako matcha tin",
    source: "curated",
  },
  kinrin: {
    src: "/images/tea/marukyu/kinrin.webp",
    fit: "contain",
    position: "center",
    altVi: "Hộp Matcha Kinrin của Marukyu Koyamaen",
    altEn: "Marukyu Koyamaen Kinrin matcha tin",
    source: "curated",
  },
  unkaku: {
    src: "/images/tea/marukyu/unkaku.webp",
    fit: "contain",
    position: "center",
    altVi: "Hộp Matcha Unkaku của Marukyu Koyamaen",
    altEn: "Marukyu Koyamaen Unkaku matcha tin",
    source: "curated",
  },
};

const legacyCuratedPaths: Record<string, string[]> = {
  isuzu: ["/images/tea/marukyu/isuzu.jpg"],
  "chigi-no-shiro": ["/images/tea/marukyu/chigi-no-shiro.jpg"],
  yugen: ["/images/tea/marukyu/yugen.jpg"],
  "wako-marukyu-koyamaen": ["/images/tea/marukyu/wako.jpg"],
  kinrin: ["/images/tea/marukyu/kinrin.jpg"],
  unkaku: ["/images/tea/marukyu/unkaku.jpg"],
};

export const HOME_MARUKYU_SLUGS = [
  "isuzu",
  "chigi-no-shiro",
  "yugen",
  "wako-marukyu-koyamaen",
  "kinrin",
  "unkaku",
] as const;

function isLegacySeedVisual(url: string | null | undefined) {
  return !url || /^\/images\/tea\/[^/]+\.svg$/i.test(url);
}

function usesCuratedLocalAsset(product: TeaProduct, curated: ProductMedia) {
  if (product.image_url === curated.src) return true;
  return legacyCuratedPaths[product.slug]?.includes(product.image_url ?? "") ?? false;
}

export function resolveProductMedia(product: TeaProduct): ProductMedia | null {
  const curated = curatedProductMedia[product.slug];

  // Admin/Cloudinary media always wins. Legacy SVG/JPG seed visuals are upgraded
  // to the curated WebP photography supplied for the Marukyu homepage set.
  if (curated && (isLegacySeedVisual(product.image_url) || usesCuratedLocalAsset(product, curated))) {
    return curated;
  }

  if (product.image_url && !isLegacySeedVisual(product.image_url)) {
    return {
      src: optimizeCloudinaryUrl(product.image_url, 900) || product.image_url,
      fit: "cover",
      position: "center",
      altVi: `${product.name} - hình ảnh sản phẩm`,
      altEn: `${product.name} product image`,
      source: "product",
    };
  }

  if (curated) return curated;
  if (!product.image_url) return null;

  return {
    src: product.image_url,
    fit: "cover",
    position: "center",
    altVi: `${product.name} - hình ảnh tham khảo`,
    altEn: `${product.name} reference image`,
    source: "product",
  };
}

export function getHomepageFeaturedMatcha(products: TeaProduct[], limit = 6) {
  const bySlug = new Map(products.map((item) => [item.slug, item]));
  const curated = HOME_MARUKYU_SLUGS
    .map((slug) => bySlug.get(slug))
    .filter((item): item is TeaProduct => Boolean(item && item.is_visible));

  // Keep one coherent photographed tea-house set on the homepage.
  if (curated.length >= Math.min(limit, 4)) {
    return curated.slice(0, limit);
  }

  const seen = new Set(curated.map((item) => item.id));
  const fallback = [
    ...products.filter((item) => item.is_featured && item.is_visible && !seen.has(item.id)),
    ...products.filter((item) => item.is_visible && !item.is_featured && !seen.has(item.id)),
  ];

  return [...curated, ...fallback].slice(0, limit);
}

================
File: lib/data/repository.ts
================
import { unstable_cache } from "next/cache";
import { getPublicSupabase } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { getSeedBrandBySlug, getSeedProductBySlug, teaBrands, teaProducts } from "@/lib/data/seed";
import type { TeaBrand, TeaProduct } from "@/lib/types/tea";

const PUBLIC_REVALIDATE_SECONDS = 180;

function attachBrands(products: TeaProduct[], brands: TeaBrand[]) {
  return products.map((product) => ({
    ...product,
    brand: brands.find((brand) => brand.id === product.brand_id) ?? product.brand ?? null,
  }));
}

async function loadTeaBrands(): Promise<TeaBrand[]> {
  const supabase = getPublicSupabase();
  if (!supabase) return teaBrands.filter((item) => item.is_visible);

  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from("tea_brands")
    .select("*")
    .eq("is_visible", true)
    .order("sort_order", { ascending: true });

  if (error || !data?.length) return teaBrands.filter((item) => item.is_visible);
  return data as TeaBrand[];
}

const getCachedTeaBrands = unstable_cache(
  loadTeaBrands,
  ["matcha-archive-public-brands-v1"],
  {
    revalidate: PUBLIC_REVALIDATE_SECONDS,
    tags: ["tea-brands"],
  },
);

export async function getTeaBrands(): Promise<TeaBrand[]> {
  return getCachedTeaBrands();
}

async function loadTeaProducts(teaType: string | null): Promise<TeaProduct[]> {
  const supabase = getPublicSupabase();

  if (!supabase) {
    return teaProducts.filter(
      (item) => item.is_visible && (!teaType || item.tea_type === teaType),
    );
  }

  let query = supabase
    .from("tea_products")
    .select("*, brand:tea_brands(*)")
    .eq("is_visible", true)
    .order("sort_order", { ascending: true });

  if (teaType) query = query.eq("tea_type", teaType);

  const { data, error } = await query;

  if (error || !data?.length) {
    return attachBrands(
      teaProducts.filter(
        (item) => item.is_visible && (!teaType || item.tea_type === teaType),
      ),
      teaBrands.filter((item) => item.is_visible),
    );
  }

  return data as TeaProduct[];
}

const getCachedTeaProducts = unstable_cache(
  loadTeaProducts,
  ["matcha-archive-public-products-v1"],
  {
    revalidate: PUBLIC_REVALIDATE_SECONDS,
    tags: ["tea-products", "tea-brands"],
  },
);

export async function getTeaProducts(options?: { teaType?: string }): Promise<TeaProduct[]> {
  return getCachedTeaProducts(options?.teaType ?? null);
}

async function loadTeaProductBySlug(slug: string): Promise<TeaProduct | null> {
  const supabase = getPublicSupabase();
  if (!supabase) return getSeedProductBySlug(slug);

  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from("tea_products")
    .select("*, brand:tea_brands(*)")
    .eq("slug", slug)
    .eq("is_visible", true)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (error || !data) return getSeedProductBySlug(slug);
  return data as TeaProduct;
}

const getCachedTeaProductBySlug = unstable_cache(
  loadTeaProductBySlug,
  ["matcha-archive-public-product-by-slug-v1"],
  {
    revalidate: PUBLIC_REVALIDATE_SECONDS,
    tags: ["tea-products", "tea-brands"],
  },
);

export async function getTeaProductBySlug(slug: string): Promise<TeaProduct | null> {
  return getCachedTeaProductBySlug(slug);
}

async function loadTeaBrandBySlug(slug: string): Promise<TeaBrand | null> {
  const supabase = getPublicSupabase();
  if (!supabase) return getSeedBrandBySlug(slug);

  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from("tea_brands")
    .select("*")
    .eq("slug", slug)
    .eq("is_visible", true)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (error || !data) return getSeedBrandBySlug(slug);
  return data as TeaBrand;
}

const getCachedTeaBrandBySlug = unstable_cache(
  loadTeaBrandBySlug,
  ["matcha-archive-public-brand-by-slug-v1"],
  {
    revalidate: PUBLIC_REVALIDATE_SECONDS,
    tags: ["tea-brands"],
  },
);

export async function getTeaBrandBySlug(slug: string): Promise<TeaBrand | null> {
  return getCachedTeaBrandBySlug(slug);
}

export async function getProductsForBrand(brandId: string) {
  const all = await getTeaProducts();
  return all.filter((product) => product.brand_id === brandId);
}

import type { SiteContentRecord } from "@/lib/types/tea";

type LocalizedBlock = { title?: string; body?: string };
type I18nPayload = { i18n?: { vi?: LocalizedBlock; en?: LocalizedBlock } };

export function resolveAdminContent(
  record: SiteContentRecord | null,
  defaults: { titleVi: string; bodyVi: string; titleEn: string; bodyEn: string },
) {
  const payload = (record?.payload ?? {}) as I18nPayload;
  return {
    titleVi: payload.i18n?.vi?.title ?? record?.title ?? defaults.titleVi,
    bodyVi: payload.i18n?.vi?.body ?? record?.body ?? defaults.bodyVi,
    titleEn: payload.i18n?.en?.title ?? defaults.titleEn,
    bodyEn: payload.i18n?.en?.body ?? defaults.bodyEn,
  };
}

================
File: lib/i18n/content.ts
================
export type AppLocale = "vi" | "en";

export function normalizeLocale(value?: string | null): AppLocale {
  return value === "en" ? "en" : "vi";
}

export function localizedText(
  locale: string | undefined,
  vietnamese: string | null | undefined,
  english: string | null | undefined,
  fallback = "",
) {
  const lng = normalizeLocale(locale);
  if (lng === "en") return english || vietnamese || fallback;
  return vietnamese || english || fallback;
}

================
File: lib/i18n/routing.ts
================
export const supportedLocales = ["vi", "en"] as const;
export type AppLocale = (typeof supportedLocales)[number];
export const defaultLocale: AppLocale = "vi";

export function normalizeLocale(value?: string | null): AppLocale {
  return value === "en" ? "en" : "vi";
}

export function stripLocalePrefix(path: string) {
  const value = path || "/";
  return value.replace(/^\/(vi|en)(?=\/|$)/, "") || "/";
}

export function withLocalePath(path: string, locale?: string | null) {
  if (!path) return normalizeLocale(locale) === "en" ? "/en" : "/";
  if (/^(https?:|mailto:|tel:)/i.test(path) || path.startsWith("#")) return path;

  const normalized = normalizeLocale(locale);
  const hashIndex = path.indexOf("#");
  const hash = hashIndex >= 0 ? path.slice(hashIndex) : "";
  const withoutHash = hashIndex >= 0 ? path.slice(0, hashIndex) : path;
  const queryIndex = withoutHash.indexOf("?");
  const query = queryIndex >= 0 ? withoutHash.slice(queryIndex) : "";
  const pathname = queryIndex >= 0 ? withoutHash.slice(0, queryIndex) : withoutHash;
  const clean = stripLocalePrefix(pathname.startsWith("/") ? pathname : `/${pathname}`);
  const localized = normalized === "en" ? `/en${clean === "/" ? "" : clean}` : clean;
  return `${localized || "/"}${query}${hash}`;
}

export function localeAlternates(path: string, locale?: string | null) {
  return {
    canonical: withLocalePath(path, locale),
    languages: {
      vi: withLocalePath(path, "vi"),
      en: withLocalePath(path, "en"),
    },
  };
}

import "server-only";
import { createClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";

export function getAdminSupabase() {
  const url = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
  const key = process.env.SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY ?? process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;
  if (!url || !key) return null;
  return createClient(url, key, {
    auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false },
  });
}

export function formatReferencePrice(price: number | null, unit: string | null) {
  if (price == null) return "Chưa cập nhật";
  return `${new Intl.NumberFormat("vi-VN").format(price)}đ${unit ? ` / ${unit}` : ""}`;
}

export function cn(...values: Array<string | false | null | undefined>) {
  return values.filter(Boolean).join(" ");
}

================
File: MIE-MATCHA-FEATURED-CAROUSEL-v4.5.1/find-section.mjs
================
export function findSectionByAria(source, ariaId) {
  const needle = `aria-labelledby="${ariaId}"`;
  const ariaIndex = source.indexOf(needle);
  if (ariaIndex < 0) {
    throw new Error(`Could not find ${needle}`);
  }

  const start = source.lastIndexOf('<section', ariaIndex);
  if (start < 0) {
    throw new Error(`Could not find opening <section> for ${ariaId}`);
  }

  const tagRe = /<\/?section\b[^>]*>/g;
  tagRe.lastIndex = start;
  let depth = 0;
  let match;

  while ((match = tagRe.exec(source))) {
    const token = match[0];
    if (token.startsWith('</section')) {
      depth -= 1;
      if (depth === 0) {
        const end = tagRe.lastIndex;
        return { start, end, text: source.slice(start, end) };
      }
    } else {
      depth += 1;
    }
  }

  throw new Error(`Could not find closing </section> for ${ariaId}`);
}

$nodePatch = Join-Path $scriptDir 'patch-featured-v4.5.1.mjs'
if (!(Test-Path -LiteralPath $nodePatch)) {
  throw 'Missing Node patcher.'
}

node $nodePatch --root $root
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
  throw 'Node patcher failed.'
}

Remove-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $root '.next') -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
Remove-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $root 'tsconfig.tsbuildinfo') -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

if (!$SkipChecks) {
  npm run verify
  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'npm run verify failed' }

  npm run typecheck
  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'npm run typecheck failed' }
}

Write-Host ''

Write-Host 'Run: npm run dev'

import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { findSectionByAria } from "./find-section.mjs";

const args = process.argv.slice(2);
const rootIndex = args.indexOf("--root");
if (rootIndex < 0 || !args[rootIndex + 1]) {
  throw new Error("Missing --root argument.");
}

const root = path.resolve(args[rootIndex + 1]);
const patchDir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const homePagePath = path.join(root, "components", "home", "HomePage.tsx");
const targetInteractiveDir = path.join(root, "components", "home", "interactive");
const targetShowcasePath = path.join(targetInteractiveDir, "FeaturedShowcase.tsx");
const targetShowcaseCssPath = path.join(targetInteractiveDir, "FeaturedShowcase.module.css");
const sourceShowcasePath = path.join(patchDir, "payload", "components", "home", "interactive", "FeaturedShowcase.tsx.txt");
const sourceShowcaseCssPath = path.join(patchDir, "payload", "components", "home", "interactive", "FeaturedShowcase.module.css.txt");

const targetAssetA = path.join(targetAssetDir, "matcha-pour.jpg");
const targetAssetB = path.join(targetAssetDir, "matcha-origin-table.jpg");

for (const file of [homePagePath, sourceShowcasePath, sourceShowcaseCssPath, sourceAssetA, sourceAssetB]) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) {
    throw new Error(`Missing required file: ${file}`);
  }

// Phase 1 diagnostic: prove the section exists before touching any production file.

  featuredSection = findSectionByAria(source, "featured-title");
} catch (error) {
  const hasProductGrid = source.includes("styles.productGrid");
  const hasFeaturedText = source.includes("featuredTitle");
  console.error("FEATURED SECTION DIAGNOSTIC");
  console.error(`aria-labelledby=featured-title: NOT FOUND`);
  console.error(`styles.productGrid present: ${hasProductGrid}`);
  console.error(`featuredTitle present: ${hasFeaturedText}`);
  throw error;
}

console.log(`Featured section located at characters ${featuredSection.start}-${featuredSection.end}.`);

const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:T]/g, "").slice(0, 14);
const backupDir = path.join(root, `.backup-featured-carousel-v4.5.1-${stamp}`);
fs.mkdirSync(path.join(backupDir, "components", "home", "interactive"), { recursive: true });
fs.copyFileSync(homePagePath, path.join(backupDir, "components", "home", "HomePage.tsx"));
if (fs.existsSync(targetShowcasePath)) {
  fs.copyFileSync(targetShowcasePath, path.join(backupDir, "components", "home", "interactive", "FeaturedShowcase.tsx"));
}
if (fs.existsSync(targetShowcaseCssPath)) {
  fs.copyFileSync(targetShowcaseCssPath, path.join(backupDir, "components", "home", "interactive", "FeaturedShowcase.module.css"));
}
console.log(`Backup: ${backupDir}`);

fs.mkdirSync(targetInteractiveDir, { recursive: true });
fs.copyFileSync(sourceShowcasePath, targetShowcasePath);
fs.copyFileSync(sourceShowcaseCssPath, targetShowcaseCssPath);
fs.mkdirSync(targetAssetDir, { recursive: true });
fs.copyFileSync(sourceAssetA, targetAssetA);
fs.copyFileSync(sourceAssetB, targetAssetB);

if (!source.includes(importLine)) {
  const stylesImport = 'import styles from "./HomePage.module.css";';
  if (!source.includes(stylesImport)) {
    throw new Error("Could not locate HomePage.module.css import.");
  }
  source = source.replace(stylesImport, `${stylesImport}\n${importLine}`);
}

const dataEnd = "  // MIE MATCHA FEATURED CAROUSEL V4.5.1 END";

    },

      src: "/images/home/featured/matcha-origin-table.jpg",

    return {

            position: productMedia.position,
            fit: productMedia.fit,
          }
        : undefined,
    };
  });
  ${dataEnd}`;

if (source.includes(dataStart)) {
  const start = source.indexOf(dataStart);
  const end = source.indexOf(dataEnd, start);
  if (end < 0) throw new Error("Existing carousel marker is incomplete.");
  source = source.slice(0, start) + dataBlock + source.slice(end + dataEnd.length);
} else {
  const homeFn = source.indexOf("export async function HomePage");
  if (homeFn < 0) throw new Error("Could not find HomePage function.");
  const returnIndex = source.indexOf("\n  return (", homeFn);
  if (returnIndex < 0) throw new Error("Could not find HomePage return statement.");
  source = source.slice(0, returnIndex) + `\n\n${dataBlock}\n` + source.slice(returnIndex);
}

// Re-find after inserting data because character offsets changed.
featuredSection = findSectionByAria(source, "featured-title");
const replacement = `      <FeaturedShowcase
        libraryHref={withLocalePath("/matcha", lng)}
        eyebrow={editorialLabel(c.featuredEyebrow, vi)}
        title={c.featuredTitle}

        slides={showcaseSlides}
        products={showcaseProducts}
      />`;

source = source.slice(0, featuredSection.start) + replacement + source.slice(featuredSection.end);

if (source.includes("<section") && !source.includes("<FeaturedShowcase")) {
  throw new Error("FeaturedShowcase replacement did not apply.");
}

const replacementChar = String.fromCharCode(0xfffd);
if (source.includes(replacementChar)) {
  throw new Error("Unicode replacement character detected after patching.");
}

File: MIE-MATCHA-FEATURED-CAROUSEL-v4.5.1/payload/components/home/interactive/FeaturedShowcase.tsx.txt
================
"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, ArrowUpRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useKeenSlider } from "keen-slider/react";
import styles from "./FeaturedShowcase.module.css";

type HeroSlide = {
  src: string;
  alt: string;
  eyebrow: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
};

type ProductSlide = {
  id: string;
  href: string;
  name: string;
  japaneseName?: string;
  brandName: string;
  summary: string;
  origin: string;
  price?: string | null;
  image?: {
    src: string;
    alt: string;
    position?: string;
    fit?: "contain" | "cover";
  };
};

type FeaturedShowcaseProps = {
  libraryHref: string;
  eyebrow: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  ctaLabel: string;
  exploreLabel: string;
  referencePriceLabel: string;
  autoSwitchLabel: string;
  dragLabel: string;
  slides: HeroSlide[];
  products: ProductSlide[];
};

export function FeaturedShowcase({
  libraryHref,
  eyebrow,
  title,
  description,
  ctaLabel,
  exploreLabel,
  referencePriceLabel,
  autoSwitchLabel,
  dragLabel,
  slides,
  products,
}: FeaturedShowcaseProps) {
  const [heroIndex, setHeroIndex] = useState(0);
  const [productIndex, setProductIndex] = useState(0);
  const [productMaxIndex, setProductMaxIndex] = useState(0);

  const heroAutoplay = useMemo(
    () => (slider: any) => {
      let timeout: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
      let paused = false;

      const clear = () => {
        if (timeout) clearTimeout(timeout);
      };

      const schedule = () => {
        clear();
        if (paused) return;
        timeout = setTimeout(() => slider.next(), 4800);
      };

      const pause = () => {
        paused = true;
        clear();
      };

      const resume = () => {
        paused = false;
        schedule();
      };

      slider.on("created", () => {
        slider.container.addEventListener("mouseenter", pause);
        slider.container.addEventListener("mouseleave", resume);
        slider.container.addEventListener("focusin", pause);
        slider.container.addEventListener("focusout", resume);
        schedule();
      });
      slider.on("dragStarted", clear);
      slider.on("animationEnded", schedule);
      slider.on("updated", schedule);
      slider.on("destroyed", () => {
        clear();
        slider.container.removeEventListener("mouseenter", pause);
        slider.container.removeEventListener("mouseleave", resume);
        slider.container.removeEventListener("focusin", pause);
        slider.container.removeEventListener("focusout", resume);
      });
    },
    []
  );

  const [heroRef, heroSlider] = useKeenSlider<HTMLDivElement>(
    {
      loop: slides.length > 1,
      rubberband: false,
      slides: { perView: 1, spacing: 0 },
      created(slider) {
        setHeroIndex(slider.track.details.rel);
      },
      slideChanged(slider) {
        setHeroIndex(slider.track.details.rel);
      },
    },
    [heroAutoplay]
  );

  const [productRef, productSlider] = useKeenSlider<HTMLDivElement>({
    loop: false,
    rubberband: true,
    renderMode: "performance",
    slides: { perView: 1.08, spacing: 14 },
    breakpoints: {
      "(min-width: 720px)": {
        slides: { perView: 1.28, spacing: 18 },
      },
      "(min-width: 1050px)": {
        slides: { perView: 1.42, spacing: 20 },
      },
      "(min-width: 1500px)": {
        slides: { perView: 2.02, spacing: 22 },
      },
    },
    created(slider) {
      setProductIndex(slider.track.details.rel);
      setProductMaxIndex(slider.track.details.maxIdx);
    },
    updated(slider) {
      setProductIndex(slider.track.details.rel);
      setProductMaxIndex(slider.track.details.maxIdx);
    },
    slideChanged(slider) {
      setProductIndex(slider.track.details.rel);
      setProductMaxIndex(slider.track.details.maxIdx);
    },
  });

  return (
    <section className={styles.section} aria-labelledby="featured-showcase-title">
      <div className={styles.shell}>
        <div className={styles.layout}>
          <div className={styles.mediaPane}>
            <div ref={heroRef} className={`${styles.keen} ${styles.heroSlider}`}>
              {slides.map((slide, index) => (
                <article key={`${slide.src}-${index}`} className={`${styles.keenSlide} ${styles.heroSlide}`}>
                  <Image
                    src={slide.src}
                    alt={slide.alt}
                    fill
                    priority={index === 0}
                    quality={90}
                    sizes="(max-width: 1049px) 100vw, 42vw"
                    className={styles.heroImage}
                  />
                  <span className={styles.heroShade} aria-hidden="true" />
                  <div className={styles.heroCopy}>
                    <p className={styles.heroEyebrow}>{slide.eyebrow}</p>
                    <h2>{slide.title}</h2>
                    <p>{slide.description}</p>
                    <Link href={libraryHref} className={styles.heroCta}>
                      {ctaLabel}
                      <ArrowUpRight size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
                    </Link>
                  </div>
                </article>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className={styles.heroFooter}>
              <div className={styles.heroDots} aria-label="Hero slides">
                {slides.map((slide, index) => (
                  <button
                    key={`${slide.src}-dot`}
                    type="button"
                    aria-label={`Slide ${index + 1}`}
                    aria-current={heroIndex === index ? "true" : undefined}
                    className={`${styles.heroDot} ${heroIndex === index ? styles.heroDotActive : ""}`}
                    onClick={() => heroSlider.current?.moveToIdx(index)}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
              <span>{autoSwitchLabel}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className={styles.productPane}>
            <header className={styles.productHeader}>
              <div>
                <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{eyebrow}</p>
                <h2 id="featured-showcase-title">{title}</h2>
              </div>
              <div className={styles.productIntro}>
                <p>{description}</p>
                <div className={styles.productTools}>
                  <span>{dragLabel}</span>
                  <div className={styles.navButtons}>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      aria-label="Previous product"
                      disabled={productIndex <= 0}
                      onClick={() => productSlider.current?.prev()}
                    >
                      <ArrowLeft size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      aria-label="Next product"
                      disabled={productIndex >= productMaxIndex}
                      onClick={() => productSlider.current?.next()}
                    >
                      <ArrowRight size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </header>

            <div ref={productRef} className={`${styles.keen} ${styles.productSlider}`}>
              {products.map((product) => (
                <div key={product.id} className={`${styles.keenSlide} ${styles.productSlide}`}>
                  <Link href={product.href} className={styles.productCard} aria-label={`${exploreLabel}: ${product.name}`}>
                    <figure className={styles.productMedia}>
                      <div className={styles.productMediaInner}>
                        {product.image ? (
                          <Image
                            src={product.image.src}
                            alt={product.image.alt}
                            fill
                            quality={90}
                            sizes="(max-width: 719px) 78vw, (max-width: 1499px) 42vw, 26vw"
                            className={product.image.fit === "cover" ? styles.productImageCover : styles.productImageContain}
                            style={{ objectPosition: product.image.position || "50% 50%" }}
                          />
                        ) : (
                          <span className={styles.productFallback}>{product.japaneseName || product.name}</span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </figure>

                    <div className={styles.productBody}>
                      <p className={styles.productBrand}>{product.brandName}</p>
                      <div className={styles.productHeading}>
                        <div>
                          {product.japaneseName ? <span>{product.japaneseName}</span> : null}
                          <h3>{product.name}</h3>
                        </div>
                        <ArrowUpRight size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
                      </div>
                      {product.summary ? <p className={styles.productSummary}>{product.summary}</p> : null}
                      <div className={styles.productMeta}>
                        <span>{product.origin}</span>
                        {product.price ? <span>{referencePriceLabel}: {product.price}</span> : null}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </Link>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className={styles.productFooter}>
              <span>{String(productIndex + 1).padStart(2, "0")} / {String(products.length).padStart(2, "0")}</span>
              <Link href={libraryHref}>
                {ctaLabel}
                <ArrowUpRight size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

File: MIE-MATCHA-FEATURED-CAROUSEL-v4.5.1/README.md
================
# MIE MATCHA Featured Carousel v4.5.1

Fixes the fragile v4.5 installer and rebuilds the featured homepage spread:

- Left media panel is smaller and auto-switches between 3 editorial photos.
- Uses the two supplied photos plus the current homepage hero media.
- Right product area is larger and uses Keen Slider.
- Desktop shows roughly 1.4 cards, up to 2 cards on very wide screens.
- Product packaging stays large with `object-fit: contain`.
- PowerShell wrapper is ASCII-only. Unicode changes are handled by Node UTF-8.
- The installer finds the featured section by `aria-labelledby="featured-title"` and balances nested `<section>` tags instead of relying on one exact class string.

Run:

```powershell
cd D:\freetime\Matcha\matcha-archive
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\MIE-MATCHA-FEATURED-CAROUSEL-v4.5.1\PATCH-FEATURED-CAROUSEL-v4.5.1.ps1
```

File: MIE-MATCHA-FEATURED-CAROUSEL-v4.5.2/PATCH-FEATURED-CAROUSEL-v4.5.2.ps1
================
param(
  [switch]$SkipChecks
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

function Find-ProjectRoot {
  param([string]$StartPath)
  $current = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $StartPath).Path
  while ($true) {
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $current 'package.json')) { return $current }
    $parent = Split-Path -Parent $current
    if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($parent) -or $parent -eq $current) {
      throw 'Could not find project root containing package.json.'
    }
    $current = $parent
  }
}

$scriptDir = Split-Path -Parent $MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path
$root = Find-ProjectRoot -StartPath $scriptDir
Write-Host "Project root: $root"
Write-Host 'Target architecture: app/[lng]/(site)/page.tsx + home-featured-v4'
Write-Host 'Applying featured split carousel v4.5.2...'

$page = Join-Path $root 'app\[lng]\(site)\page.tsx'
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $page)) {
  throw 'Current route-level homepage file was not found: app/[lng]/(site)/page.tsx'
}

$pageText = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($page, [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8)
if (-not $pageText.Contains('home-featured-v4')) {
  Write-Host 'Current page.tsx does not contain home-featured-v4.' -ForegroundColor Red
  Write-Host 'The active homepage architecture is different from the rollback snapshot.' -ForegroundColor Yellow
  throw 'Patch stopped before changing source. Please share app/[lng]/(site)/page.tsx.'
}

node (Join-Path $scriptDir 'tests\test-locator.mjs')
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Locator regression test failed.' }

node (Join-Path $scriptDir 'patch-featured-v4.5.2.mjs') --root $root
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Node patcher failed.' }

Remove-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $root '.next') -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
Remove-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $root 'tsconfig.tsbuildinfo') -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
Write-Host 'Cleared .next and tsconfig.tsbuildinfo.'

if (-not $SkipChecks) {
  Push-Location $root
  try {
    npm run verify
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'npm run verify failed.' }
    npm run typecheck
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'npm run typecheck failed.' }
  }
  finally {
    Pop-Location
  }
}

Write-Host ''
Write-Host 'FEATURED CAROUSEL v4.5.2 APPLIED.' -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host 'Next: npm run dev'

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const args = process.argv.slice(2);
const rootIndex = args.indexOf("--root");
if (rootIndex === -1 || !args[rootIndex + 1]) throw new Error("Missing --root argument.");

const root = path.resolve(args[rootIndex + 1]);
const patchDir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const pagePath = path.join(root, "app", "[lng]", "(site)", "page.tsx");
const componentPath = path.join(root, "components", "home", "FeaturedShowcase.tsx");
const cssPath = path.join(root, "components", "home", "FeaturedShowcase.module.css");
const payloadTsx = path.join(patchDir, "payload", "components", "home", "FeaturedShowcase.tsx.txt");
const payloadCss = path.join(patchDir, "payload", "components", "home", "FeaturedShowcase.module.css.txt");
const pourSource = path.join(patchDir, "assets", "matcha-pour.jpg");
const tableSource = path.join(patchDir, "assets", "matcha-origin-table.jpg");
const pourTarget = path.join(root, "public", "images", "home", "featured", "matcha-pour.jpg");
const tableTarget = path.join(root, "public", "images", "home", "featured", "matcha-origin-table.jpg");

for (const file of [pagePath, payloadTsx, payloadCss, pourSource, tableSource]) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) throw new Error(`Missing required file: ${file}`);
}

function findSectionByClass(source, classToken) {
  const marker = `className="${classToken}"`;
  const markerIndex = source.indexOf(marker);
  if (markerIndex === -1) throw new Error(`Could not find ${marker} in app/[lng]/(site)/page.tsx`);

  const sectionStart = source.lastIndexOf("<section", markerIndex);
  if (sectionStart === -1) throw new Error("Could not find opening <section> for featured block.");

  const tagRe = /<section\b[^>]*>|<\/section>/g;
  tagRe.lastIndex = sectionStart;
  let depth = 0;
  let match;
  while ((match = tagRe.exec(source))) {
    if (match[0].startsWith("</")) depth -= 1;
    else depth += 1;
    if (depth === 0) return { start: sectionStart, end: tagRe.lastIndex };
  }
  throw new Error("Could not find closing </section> for featured block.");
}

function writeUtf8(file, content) {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(file, content.normalize("NFC"), "utf8");
}

const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:T]/g, "").slice(0, 14);
const backup = path.join(root, `.backup-featured-carousel-v4.5.2-${stamp}`);
fs.mkdirSync(backup, { recursive: true });
fs.mkdirSync(path.join(backup, "app", "[lng]", "(site)"), { recursive: true });
fs.copyFileSync(pagePath, path.join(backup, "app", "[lng]", "(site)", "page.tsx"));
for (const file of [componentPath, cssPath]) {
  if (fs.existsSync(file)) {
    const target = path.join(backup, "components", "home", path.basename(file));
    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(target), { recursive: true });
    fs.copyFileSync(file, target);
  }
}
console.log(`Backup: ${backup}`);

fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(pourTarget), { recursive: true });
fs.copyFileSync(pourSource, pourTarget);
fs.copyFileSync(tableSource, tableTarget);

console.log("Added: public/images/home/featured/matcha-origin-table.jpg");

writeUtf8(componentPath, fs.readFileSync(payloadTsx, "utf8"));
writeUtf8(cssPath, fs.readFileSync(payloadCss, "utf8"));
console.log("Updated: components/home/FeaturedShowcase.tsx");

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, ArrowUpRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useKeenSlider } from "keen-slider/react";
import type { TeaProduct } from "@/lib/types/tea";
import { localizedText } from "@/lib/i18n/content";
import { resolveProductMedia } from "@/lib/data/product-media";
import { teaProfileHref } from "@/lib/routes";
import { withLocalePath } from "@/lib/i18n/routing";
import styles from "./FeaturedShowcase.module.css";

const AUTOPLAY_MS = 4800;

const FEATURE_IMAGES = [
  "/images/home/featured/matcha-pour.jpg",
  "/images/home/featured/matcha-origin-table.jpg",
  "/images/home/user/hero-ceremony.png",
] as const;

type Props = {
  products: TeaProduct[];
  lng: string;
  eyebrow: string;
  title: string;
  introCopy: string;
  openLibraryLabel: string;
  exploreLabel: string;
};

export function FeaturedShowcase({
  products,
  lng,
  eyebrow,
  title,
  introCopy,
  openLibraryLabel,
  exploreLabel,
}: Props) {
  const vi = lng === "vi" || lng.startsWith("vi-");
  const [heroIndex, setHeroIndex] = useState(0);
  const [productIndex, setProductIndex] = useState(0);

  const heroSlides = useMemo(
    () => [
      {
        src: FEATURE_IMAGES[0],
        eyebrow: vi ? "MIE MATCHA / NGHI THỨC" : "MIE MATCHA / RITUAL",
        title: vi ? "Một chuyển động chậm hơn." : "A slower movement.",
        description: vi
          ? "Từ lúc rót trà đến khi chạm môi, mỗi thao tác đều là một phần của trải nghiệm vị."
          : "From the pour to the first sip, each movement becomes part of the tasting experience.",
        position: "50% 50%",
      },
      {
        src: FEATURE_IMAGES[1],
        eyebrow: vi ? "MIE MATCHA / NGUỒN TRÀ" : "MIE MATCHA / TEA SOURCE",
        title: vi ? "Nhìn gần hơn vào thế giới phía sau chén trà." : "A closer look at the world behind the bowl.",
        description: vi
          ? "Nhà trà, bao bì, dụng cụ và những dấu vết nhỏ tạo nên bối cảnh cho từng profile."
          : "Tea houses, packaging, tools and small details provide context for every profile.",
        position: "50% 50%",
      },
      {
        src: FEATURE_IMAGES[2],
        eyebrow: vi ? "MIE MATCHA / TASTING JOURNAL" : "MIE MATCHA / TASTING JOURNAL",
        title: vi ? "Nghệ thuật của sự tĩnh tâm." : "The art of mindfulness.",
        description: introCopy,
        position: "50% 50%",
      },
    ],
    [introCopy, vi],
  );

  const heroAutoplay = useMemo(
    () => (slider: any) => {
      let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
      let paused = false;

      const clear = () => {
        if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
      };

      const schedule = () => {
        clear();
        if (paused) return;
        timer = setTimeout(() => slider.next(), AUTOPLAY_MS);
      };

      slider.on("created", () => {
        slider.container.addEventListener("mouseenter", () => {
          paused = true;
          clear();
        });
        slider.container.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => {
          paused = false;
          schedule();
        });
        schedule();
      });
      slider.on("dragStarted", clear);
      slider.on("animationEnded", schedule);
      slider.on("updated", schedule);
    },
    [],
  );

  const [heroRef, heroSlider] = useKeenSlider<HTMLDivElement>(
    {
      loop: true,
      renderMode: "performance",
      slides: { perView: 1, spacing: 0 },
      created(slider) {
        setHeroIndex(slider.track.details.rel);
      },
      slideChanged(slider) {
        setHeroIndex(slider.track.details.rel);
      },
    },
    [heroAutoplay],
  );

  const [productRef, productSlider] = useKeenSlider<HTMLDivElement>({
    renderMode: "performance",
    rubberband: true,
    slides: { perView: 1.08, spacing: 14 },
    breakpoints: {
      "(min-width: 720px)": {
        slides: { perView: 1.16, spacing: 16 },
      },
      "(min-width: 1050px)": {
        slides: { perView: 1.38, spacing: 18 },
      },
      "(min-width: 1500px)": {
        slides: { perView: 1.86, spacing: 20 },
      },
    },
    created(slider) {
      setProductIndex(slider.track.details.rel);
    },
    slideChanged(slider) {
      setProductIndex(slider.track.details.rel);
    },
  });

  const maxProductIndex = productSlider.current?.track.details.maxIdx ?? 0;

  return (
    <section className={styles.section} aria-labelledby="featured-showcase-title">
      <div className={styles.shell}>
        <div className={styles.layout}>
          <div className={styles.visualColumn}>
            <div ref={heroRef} className={`keen-slider ${styles.heroSlider}`}>
              {heroSlides.map((slide, index) => (
                <article key={slide.src} className={`keen-slider__slide ${styles.heroSlide}`}>
                  <Image
                    src={slide.src}
                    alt={slide.title}
                    fill
                    priority={index === 0}
                    quality={90}
                    sizes="(max-width: 1049px) 100vw, 42vw"
                    className={styles.heroImage}
                    style={{ objectPosition: slide.position }}
                  />
                  <span className={styles.heroVeil} aria-hidden="true" />
                  <div className={styles.heroCopy}>
                    <p>{slide.eyebrow}</p>
                    <h2>{slide.title}</h2>
                    <span>{slide.description}</span>
                  </div>
                </article>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className={styles.heroNav}>
              <div className={styles.heroDots} aria-label={vi ? "Chọn ảnh" : "Choose image"}>
                {heroSlides.map((slide, index) => (
                  <button
                    key={slide.src}
                    type="button"
                    className={heroIndex === index ? styles.heroDotActive : styles.heroDot}
                    onClick={() => heroSlider.current?.moveToIdx(index)}
                    aria-label={`${vi ? "Ảnh" : "Image"} ${index + 1}`}
                    aria-current={heroIndex === index ? "true" : undefined}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
              <span>{vi ? "AUTO SWITCH · 4.8S" : "AUTO SWITCH · 4.8S"}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className={styles.collectionColumn}>
            <header className={styles.collectionHeader}>
              <div>
                <p className={styles.eyebrow}>{eyebrow}</p>
                <h2 id="featured-showcase-title">{title}</h2>
              </div>
              <div className={styles.collectionIntro}>
                <p>
                  {vi
                    ? "Một tuyển chọn nhỏ để đọc profile rõ hơn. Kéo ngang để tiếp tục khám phá."
                    : "A focused selection for reading each profile more clearly. Drag horizontally to continue."}
                </p>
                <Link href={withLocalePath("/matcha", lng)}>
                  {openLibraryLabel} <ArrowUpRight size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
                </Link>
              </div>
            </header>

            <div ref={productRef} className={`keen-slider ${styles.productSlider}`}>
              {products.map((product, index) => {
                const media = resolveProductMedia(product);
                const tasting = localizedText(lng, product.tasting_summary_vi, product.tasting_summary_en);
                const href = withLocalePath(teaProfileHref(product), lng);

                return (
                  <article key={product.id} className={`keen-slider__slide ${styles.productSlide}`}>
                    <Link href={href} className={styles.productCard} aria-label={`${exploreLabel}: ${product.name}`}>
                      <figure className={styles.productMedia}>
                        <span className={styles.productIndex}>{String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0")}</span>
                        <div className={styles.productStage}>
                          {media ? (
                            <Image
                              src={media.src}
                              alt={vi ? media.altVi : media.altEn}
                              fill
                              quality={90}
                              sizes="(max-width: 719px) 82vw, (max-width: 1049px) 72vw, (max-width: 1499px) 38vw, 26vw"
                              className={media.fit === "contain" ? styles.productContain : styles.productCover}
                              style={{ objectPosition: media.position }}
                            />
                          ) : (
                            <span className={styles.productFallback}>{product.japanese_name || product.name}</span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </figure>

                      <div className={styles.productBody}>
                        <p className={styles.productBrand}>{product.brand?.name ?? "Japanese Tea"}</p>
                        {product.japanese_name ? <span className={styles.productJapanese}>{product.japanese_name}</span> : null}
                        <div className={styles.productTitleRow}>
                          <h3>{product.name}</h3>
                          <ArrowUpRight size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
                        </div>
                        <p className={styles.productTasting}>{tasting}</p>
                        <div className={styles.productFoot}>
                          <span>{product.origin_region ?? product.origin_country ?? "Japan"}</span>
                          <span>{exploreLabel}</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </Link>
                  </article>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            <footer className={styles.sliderFooter}>
              <span className={styles.dragHint}>{vi ? "KÉO ĐỂ XEM PROFILE TIẾP THEO" : "DRAG TO VIEW THE NEXT PROFILE"}</span>
              <div className={styles.counter}>
                {String(Math.min(productIndex + 1, products.length)).padStart(2, "0")} / {String(products.length).padStart(2, "0")}
              </div>
              <div className={styles.controls}>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => productSlider.current?.prev()}
                  disabled={productIndex <= 0}
                  aria-label={vi ? "Sản phẩm trước" : "Previous product"}
                >
                  <ArrowLeft size={17} aria-hidden="true" />
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => productSlider.current?.next()}
                  disabled={productIndex >= maxProductIndex}
                  aria-label={vi ? "Sản phẩm tiếp theo" : "Next product"}
                >
                  <ArrowRight size={17} aria-hidden="true" />
                </button>
              </div>
            </footer>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

================

# MIE MATCHA Featured Carousel v4.5.2

This version targets the **actual route-level homepage architecture**:

`app/[lng]/(site)/page.tsx`

The failed v4.5/v4.5.1 patches searched `components/home/HomePage.tsx`, but the active rollback architecture renders Featured Matcha directly in `app/[lng]/(site)/page.tsx` using `.home-featured-v4` and `HomeFeaturedCard`.

## UI changes
- Left visual column reduced to ~42% width.
- 3-image Keen slider with automatic switching every 4.8 seconds.
- Uses the two supplied photos plus the existing ceremony image.
- Right collection increased to ~58% width.
- Large product cards using Keen Slider.
- ~1.38 cards visible on normal desktop, ~1.86 on very wide screens.
- Drag/swipe + previous/next controls.
- Product images remain `object-fit: contain` and use `quality={90}`.

## Run

```powershell
cd D:\freetime\Matcha\matcha-archive
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass `
  -File .\MIE-MATCHA-FEATURED-CAROUSEL-v4.5.2\PATCH-FEATURED-CAROUSEL-v4.5.2.ps1
```

The installer is ASCII-only and runs a locator regression test before modifying source.

File: MIE-MATCHA-FEATURED-CAROUSEL-v4.5.2/tests/test-locator.mjs

import assert from "node:assert/strict";

function oldLocator(source) {
  return source.indexOf('aria-labelledby="featured-title"');
}

function newLocator(source) {
  const marker = 'className="section-block home-featured-v4"';
  const markerIndex = source.indexOf(marker);
  if (markerIndex === -1) return null;
  const sectionStart = source.lastIndexOf("<section", markerIndex);
  const tagRe = /<section\b[^>]*>|<\/section>/g;
  tagRe.lastIndex = sectionStart;
  let depth = 0;
  let match;
  while ((match = tagRe.exec(source))) {
    if (match[0].startsWith("</")) depth -= 1;
    else depth += 1;
    if (depth === 0) return { start: sectionStart, end: tagRe.lastIndex };
  }
  return null;
}

const fixture = `<>\n<HomeHero />\n<HomeDiscovery />\n<section className="section-block home-featured-v4">\n<div><section className="nested">Nested</section></div>\n</section>\n<TastingSpectrum />\n</>`;

assert.equal(oldLocator(fixture), -1, "old aria locator must reproduce the reported failure");
const range = newLocator(fixture);
assert.ok(range, "new locator should find route-level featured section");
const block = fixture.slice(range.start, range.end);
assert.ok(block.includes('home-featured-v4'));
assert.ok(block.includes('className="nested"'));
assert.ok(block.endsWith('</section>'));
console.log("locator regression test passed");

  throw new Error("Could not locate the current hero + collection block in components/home/HomePage.tsx.");

function findBalancedSection(text, insideIndex) {
  const start = text.lastIndexOf("<section", insideIndex);
  if (start < 0) throw new Error("Could not find opening <section> around collection marker.");

  const tagRe = /<\/?section\b[^>]*>/g;
  tagRe.lastIndex = start;
  let depth = 0;
  let match;
  while ((match = tagRe.exec(text))) {
    if (match[0].startsWith("</")) depth -= 1;
    else depth += 1;
    if (depth === 0) return { start, end: tagRe.lastIndex, text: text.slice(start, tagRe.lastIndex) };
  }
  throw new Error("Could not find closing </section> for hero collection block.");
}

const located = findBalancedSection(original, markerIndex);
if (!located.text.includes("styles.collectionPanel") || !located.text.includes("featured.map")) {
  throw new Error("Located section does not look like the expected MIE MATCHA hero/collection architecture.");
}

console.log(`Hero/collection section located: ${located.start}-${located.end}`);
if (dryRun) {
  console.log("Dry run only. No source files changed.");
  process.exit(0);
}

const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:T]/g, "").slice(0, 14);
const backup = path.join(root, `.backup-hero-collection-carousel-v4.5.3-${stamp}`);
fs.mkdirSync(path.join(backup, "components", "home", "interactive"), { recursive: true });
fs.copyFileSync(homePath, path.join(backup, "components", "home", "HomePage.tsx"));

for (const stale of [
  path.join(root, "components", "home", "interactive", "FeaturedShowcase.tsx"),
  path.join(root, "components", "home", "interactive", "FeaturedShowcase.module.css"),
]) {
  if (fs.existsSync(stale)) {
    fs.copyFileSync(stale, path.join(backup, "components", "home", "interactive", path.basename(stale)));
  }
}
if (fs.existsSync(componentTarget)) fs.copyFileSync(componentTarget, path.join(backup, "components", "home", "interactive", "HeroCollectionCarousel.tsx"));
if (fs.existsSync(cssTarget)) fs.copyFileSync(cssTarget, path.join(backup, "components", "home", "interactive", "HeroCollectionCarousel.module.css"));
console.log(`Backup: ${backup}`);

let tsx = original;

if (!tsx.includes('import { HeroCollectionCarousel } from "./interactive/HeroCollectionCarousel";')) {
  const importNeedle = 'import styles from "./HomePage.module.css";';
  if (!tsx.includes(importNeedle)) throw new Error("Could not find HomePage.module.css import anchor.");
  tsx = tsx.replace(importNeedle, `${importNeedle}\nimport { HeroCollectionCarousel } from "./interactive/HeroCollectionCarousel";`);
}

if (!tsx.includes("const heroCollectionSlides = [")) {
  const visibleRe = /(\s*const visibleBrands = [^\n]+;\r?\n)/;
  const match = tsx.match(visibleRe);
  if (!match) throw new Error("Could not find visibleBrands data anchor.");
  const dataBlock = `\n  const heroCollectionSlides = [\n    {\n      src: media.heroMain.url,\n      alt: mediaAlt(media.heroMain, vi),\n      position: media.heroMain.position,\n      eyebrow: c.heroEyebrow,\n      title: c.heroTitle,\n      description: c.heroDescription,\n    },\n    {\n      src: \"/images/home/featured/matcha-pour.webp\",\n      alt: vi ? \"Matcha được rót trong một khoảnh khắc pha trà hiện đại\" : \"Matcha being poured in a modern brewing moment\",\n      position: \"50% 45%\",\n      eyebrow: vi ? \"NGHI THỨC HIỆN ĐẠI\" : \"MODERN RITUAL\",\n      title: vi ? \"Một nhịp pha tĩnh, rõ và hiện đại.\" : \"A quieter, clearer modern ritual.\",\n      description: c.ritualDescription,\n    },\n    {\n      src: \"/images/home/featured/matcha-origin-table.webp\",\n      alt: vi ? \"Matcha và dụng cụ tại bàn pha trà\" : \"Matcha and tea tools on a preparation table\",\n      position: \"50% 48%\",\n      eyebrow: vi ? \"NGUỒN GỐC / CÂU CHUYỆN\" : \"ORIGIN / STORY\",\n      title: vi ? \"Từ nhà trà đến từng hồ sơ vị.\" : \"From tea houses to each tasting profile.\",\n      description: c.collectionDescription,\n    },\n  ];\n\n  const heroCollectionProducts = featured.map((product) => {\n    const productMedia = resolveProductMedia(product);\n    return {\n      id: String(product.id),\n      href: withLocalePath(\`/matcha/\${product.slug}\`, lng),\n      name: product.name,\n      japaneseName: product.japanese_name || \"\",\n      brandName: product.brand?.name || \"Japanese Tea\",\n      summary: getProductSummary(product, vi),\n      origin: product.origin_region || product.origin_country || \"Japan\",\n      price: formatPrice(product, vi),\n      image: productMedia\n        ? {\n            src: productMedia.src,\n            alt: vi ? productMedia.altVi : productMedia.altEn,\n            position: productMedia.position,\n            fit: productMedia.fit,\n          }\n        : undefined,\n    };\n  });\n`;
  tsx = tsx.replace(match[0], `${match[0]}${dataBlock}`);

// Re-locate after import/data insertion because character offsets changed.
let activeMarkerIndex = tsx.indexOf(marker);
if (activeMarkerIndex < 0) {
  for (const candidate of markerCandidates) {

    if (idx >= 0) {
      activeMarkerIndex = idx;
      break;
    }
  }
}
if (activeMarkerIndex < 0) throw new Error("Hero/collection marker disappeared during patch preparation.");

tsx = `${tsx.slice(0, activeSection.start)}${replacement}${tsx.slice(activeSection.end)}`;

if (tsx.includes("styles.collectionPanel") || tsx.includes("styles.collectionGrid")) {
  // These may only exist in the old block. After replacement they should be gone from TSX.
  throw new Error("Old collection markup is still present after replacement; patch aborted before writing.");
}

fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(componentTarget), { recursive: true });
fs.writeFileSync(componentTarget, fs.readFileSync(componentSource, "utf8").normalize("NFC"), "utf8");
fs.writeFileSync(cssTarget, fs.readFileSync(cssSource, "utf8").normalize("NFC"), "utf8");
fs.writeFileSync(homePath, tsx.normalize("NFC"), "utf8");

fs.mkdirSync(publicDir, { recursive: true });
for (const [src, dst] of assetPairs) fs.copyFileSync(src, dst);

for (const stale of [
  path.join(root, "components", "home", "interactive", "FeaturedShowcase.tsx"),
  path.join(root, "components", "home", "interactive", "FeaturedShowcase.module.css"),
]) {
  if (fs.existsSync(stale)) fs.rmSync(stale, { force: true });
}

console.log("Updated: components/home/HomePage.tsx");
console.log("Added: components/home/interactive/HeroCollectionCarousel.tsx");
console.log("Added: components/home/interactive/HeroCollectionCarousel.module.css");
console.log("Added: public/images/home/featured/matcha-pour.webp");

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, ArrowUpRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useKeenSlider } from "keen-slider/react";
import "keen-slider/keen-slider.min.css";
import styles from "./HeroCollectionCarousel.module.css";

type HeroSlide = {
  src: string;
  alt: string;
  position?: string;
  eyebrow: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
};

type ProductSlide = {
  id: string;
  href: string;
  name: string;
  japaneseName?: string;
  brandName: string;
  summary: string;
  origin: string;
  price?: string | null;
  image?: {
    src: string;
    alt: string;
    position?: string;
    fit?: "contain" | "cover";
  };
};

type Props = {
  heroSlides: HeroSlide[];
  products: ProductSlide[];
  exploreHref: string;
  exploreLabel: string;
  collectionEyebrow: string;
  collectionTitle: string;
  collectionDescription: string;
  exploreProfileLabel: string;
  referencePriceLabel: string;
  autoSwitchLabel: string;
  dragLabel: string;
};

function AutoSwitch(interval = 4800) {
  return (slider: any) => {
    let timeout: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
    let paused = false;

    const clear = () => {
      if (timeout) clearTimeout(timeout);
      timeout = undefined;
    };

    const schedule = () => {
      clear();
      if (paused) return;
      if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches) return;
      timeout = setTimeout(() => slider.next(), interval);
    };

    const onEnter = () => {
      paused = true;
      clear();
    };

    const onLeave = () => {
      paused = false;
      schedule();
    };

    slider.on("created", () => {
      slider.container.addEventListener("mouseenter", onEnter);
      slider.container.addEventListener("mouseleave", onLeave);
      slider.container.addEventListener("focusin", onEnter);
      slider.container.addEventListener("focusout", onLeave);
      schedule();
    });
    slider.on("dragStarted", clear);
    slider.on("animationEnded", schedule);
    slider.on("updated", schedule);
    slider.on("destroyed", () => {
      clear();
      slider.container.removeEventListener("mouseenter", onEnter);
      slider.container.removeEventListener("mouseleave", onLeave);
      slider.container.removeEventListener("focusin", onEnter);
      slider.container.removeEventListener("focusout", onLeave);
    });
  };
}

export function HeroCollectionCarousel({
  heroSlides,
  products,
  exploreHref,
  exploreLabel,
  collectionEyebrow,
  collectionTitle,
  collectionDescription,
  exploreProfileLabel,
  referencePriceLabel,
  autoSwitchLabel,
  dragLabel,
}: Props) {
  const [heroIndex, setHeroIndex] = useState(0);
  const [productIndex, setProductIndex] = useState(0);
  const [productMax, setProductMax] = useState(0);
  const autoplay = useMemo(() => AutoSwitch(4800), []);

  const [heroRef, heroInstance] = useKeenSlider<HTMLDivElement>(
    {
      loop: true,
      rubberband: false,
      renderMode: "performance",
      slides: { perView: 1, spacing: 0 },
      created(slider) {
        setHeroIndex(slider.track.details.rel);
      },
      slideChanged(slider) {
        setHeroIndex(slider.track.details.rel);
      },
    },
    [autoplay]
  );

  const [productRef, productInstance] = useKeenSlider<HTMLDivElement>({
    mode: "free-snap",
    rubberband: true,
    renderMode: "performance",
    slides: { perView: 1.08, spacing: 14 },
    breakpoints: {
      "(min-width: 720px)": {
        slides: { perView: 1.18, spacing: 16 },
      },
      "(min-width: 1050px)": {
        slides: { perView: 1.38, spacing: 18 },
      },
      "(min-width: 1500px)": {
        slides: { perView: 1.88, spacing: 20 },
      },
    },
    created(slider) {
      setProductIndex(slider.track.details.rel);
      setProductMax(slider.track.details.maxIdx);
    },
    slideChanged(slider) {
      setProductIndex(slider.track.details.rel);
      setProductMax(slider.track.details.maxIdx);
    },
    updated(slider) {
      setProductIndex(slider.track.details.rel);
      setProductMax(slider.track.details.maxIdx);
    },
  });

  return (
    <section className={styles.showcase} aria-labelledby="home-title">
      <div className={styles.heroSide}>
        <div ref={heroRef} className={`keen-slider ${styles.heroSlider}`} aria-roledescription="carousel">
          {heroSlides.map((slide, index) => (
            <article key={`${slide.src}-${index}`} className={`keen-slider__slide ${styles.heroSlide}`}>
              <Image
                src={slide.src}
                alt={slide.alt}
                fill
                priority={index === 0}
                quality={90}
                sizes="(max-width: 1049px) 100vw, 42vw"
                className={styles.heroImage}
                style={{ objectPosition: slide.position || "50% 50%" }}
              />
              <div className={styles.heroShade} aria-hidden="true" />
              <div className={styles.heroCopy}>
                <p className={styles.heroEyebrow}>{slide.eyebrow}</p>
                <h1 id={index === 0 ? "home-title" : undefined}>{slide.title}</h1>
                <p>{slide.description}</p>
                <Link href={exploreHref} className={styles.heroCta} aria-label={exploreLabel}>
                  {exploreLabel}<ArrowUpRight size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
                </Link>
              </div>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className={styles.heroControls}>
          <div className={styles.heroDots} aria-label={autoSwitchLabel}>
            {heroSlides.map((_, index) => (
              <button
                key={index}
                type="button"
                className={`${styles.heroDot} ${heroIndex === index ? styles.heroDotActive : ""}`}
                onClick={() => heroInstance.current?.moveToIdx(index)}
                aria-label={`${autoSwitchLabel} ${index + 1}`}
                aria-current={heroIndex === index ? "true" : undefined}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
          <span>{autoSwitchLabel}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <aside className={styles.collectionSide} aria-labelledby="collection-title">
        <header className={styles.collectionHeader}>
          <div>
            <p className={styles.collectionEyebrow}>{collectionEyebrow}</p>
            <h2 id="collection-title">{collectionTitle}</h2>
          </div>
          <div className={styles.collectionIntro}>
            <p>{collectionDescription}</p>
            <div className={styles.collectionTools}>
              <span>{dragLabel}</span>
              <div className={styles.navButtons}>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => productInstance.current?.prev()}
                  disabled={productIndex <= 0}
                  aria-label="Previous matcha profiles"
                >
                  <ArrowLeft size={16} />
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => productInstance.current?.next()}
                  disabled={productIndex >= productMax}
                  aria-label="Next matcha profiles"
                >
                  <ArrowRight size={16} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </header>

        <div ref={productRef} className={`keen-slider ${styles.productSlider}`} aria-roledescription="carousel">
          {products.map((product) => (
            <div key={product.id} className={`keen-slider__slide ${styles.productSlide}`}>
              <Link href={product.href} className={styles.productCard} aria-label={`${exploreProfileLabel}: ${product.name}`}>
                <figure className={styles.productMedia}>
                  <div className={styles.productStage}>
                    {product.image ? (
                      <Image
                        src={product.image.src}
                        alt={product.image.alt}
                        fill
                        quality={90}
                        sizes="(max-width: 719px) 86vw, (max-width: 1049px) 70vw, (max-width: 1499px) 38vw, 28vw"
                        className={product.image.fit === "cover" ? styles.productImageCover : styles.productImageContain}
                        style={{ objectPosition: product.image.position || "50% 50%" }}
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <span className={styles.productFallback}>{product.japaneseName || product.name}</span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </figure>

                <div className={styles.productCopy}>
                  <p className={styles.productBrand}>{product.brandName}</p>
                  <div className={styles.productHeading}>
                    <div>
                      {product.japaneseName ? <span>{product.japaneseName}</span> : null}
                      <h3>{product.name}</h3>
                    </div>
                    <ArrowUpRight size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
                  </div>
                  {product.summary ? <p className={styles.productSummary}>{product.summary}</p> : null}
                  <div className={styles.productFooter}>
                    <span>{product.origin}</span>
                    {product.price ? <strong>{referencePriceLabel}: {product.price}</strong> : null}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </Link>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className={styles.productProgress} aria-hidden="true">
          <span>{String(Math.min(productIndex + 1, Math.max(products.length, 1))).padStart(2, "0")}</span>
          <i><b style={{ width: `${products.length > 1 ? ((productIndex + 1) / products.length) * 100 : 100}%` }} /></i>
          <span>{String(products.length).padStart(2, "0")}</span>
        </div>
      </aside>
    </section>
  );
}

# MIE MATCHA Hero + Collection Carousel v4.5.3

Targets the current mindful editorial homepage architecture in `components/home/HomePage.tsx`, specifically the split `heroCollection` containing `heroPanel` and `collectionPanel`.

Changes:
- Left side becomes an auto-switching Keen Slider using the current hero plus the two supplied photos.
- Left/right ratio becomes approximately 42/58 on desktop.
- Right side becomes a large product Keen Slider instead of a 3 x 2 product grid.
- Desktop shows about 1.4 large cards, up to about 1.9 on very wide screens.
- Drag/swipe and previous/next controls are available.
- Product packaging uses `object-fit: contain` on a soft matcha stage.
- Removes stale `FeaturedShowcase` files left by failed v4.5 attempts after backing them up.

## Recommended first run

```powershell
cd D:\freetime\Matcha\matcha-archive
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\MIE-MATCHA-HERO-COLLECTION-CAROUSEL-v4.5.3\PATCH-HERO-COLLECTION-CAROUSEL-v4.5.3.ps1 -DryRun
```

If diagnostic reports `styles.heroCollection: true` and `styles.collectionPanel: true`, apply:

```powershell
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\MIE-MATCHA-HERO-COLLECTION-CAROUSEL-v4.5.3\PATCH-HERO-COLLECTION-CAROUSEL-v4.5.3.ps1
```

param(
  [switch]$SkipChecks
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

function Find-ProjectRoot {
  param([string]$StartPath)
  $current = (Resolve-Path $StartPath).Path
  while ($true) {
    if (Test-Path (Join-Path $current 'package.json')) { return $current }
    $parent = Split-Path $current -Parent
    if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($parent) -or $parent -eq $current) {
      throw 'Could not find project root containing package.json.'
    }
    $current = $parent
  }
}

$scriptDir = Split-Path -Parent $MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path
$root = Find-ProjectRoot $scriptDir
$patcher = Join-Path $scriptDir 'patch-journal-v4.3.mjs'

if (-not (Test-Path $patcher)) { throw "Missing patcher: $patcher" }

Write-Host "Project root: $root" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host 'Applying journal layout patch...' -ForegroundColor DarkGray

& node $patcher --root $root
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Node patcher failed.' }

Remove-Item (Join-Path $root '.next') -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
Remove-Item (Join-Path $root 'tsconfig.tsbuildinfo') -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

if (-not $SkipChecks) {
  Push-Location $root
  try {
    npm run verify
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'npm run verify failed' }

    npm run typecheck
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'npm run typecheck failed' }
  }
  finally {
    Pop-Location
  }
}

Write-Host ''
Write-Host 'JOURNAL MINDFUL V4.3 COMPLETED.' -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host 'Run npm run dev, then hard refresh the homepage.' -ForegroundColor DarkGray

  .journalHeading h2 { font-size: clamp(2.4rem, 5vw, 3.65rem); }
  .journalHeroMedia { min-height: clamp(28rem, 50vw, 36rem); }
  .journalReadingGrid { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); }
}

  .journalMagazine { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
  .journalCopyColumn { gap: 1.15rem; }
  .journalHeading h2 { max-width: 12ch; font-size: clamp(2.35rem, 12vw, 3.5rem); line-height: .98; }

  .journalFeatureNote h3 { max-width: 13ch; font-size: clamp(1.65rem, 8vw, 2.25rem); }
  .journalHeroMedia { min-height: 0; aspect-ratio: 4 / 5; }
  .journalHeroCaption { right: .65rem; bottom: .65rem; }
  .journalReadingGrid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
  .journalReadingCard { min-height: 8.75rem; grid-template-columns: 4.8rem minmax(0, 1fr) auto; padding: .7rem; }
  .journalReadingThumb { min-height: 7.25rem; }
}

@media (max-width: 430px) {
  .journalReadingCard { grid-template-columns: 4.2rem minmax(0, 1fr) auto; gap: .7rem; }
  .journalReadingThumb { min-height: 6.6rem; }
  .journalReadingCard p { font-size: .6rem; }
}
/* MIE MATCHA JOURNAL MINDFUL V4.3 END */`;

const markerRe = new RegExp(`${startMarker.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&")}[\\s\\S]*?${endMarker.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&")}`);
if (markerRe.test(css)) {
  css = css.replace(markerRe, block);
} else {
  css = `${css.trimEnd()}\n\n${block}\n`;
}

const replacementChar = String.fromCharCode(0xfffd);
if (tsx.includes(replacementChar) || css.includes(replacementChar)) {
  throw new Error("Unicode replacement character detected after patching.");
}

fs.writeFileSync(tsxPath, tsx.normalize("NFC"), "utf8");
fs.writeFileSync(cssPath, css.normalize("NFC"), "utf8");
console.log("Updated: components/home/HomePage.tsx");
console.log("Updated: components/home/HomePage.module.css");
console.log("UTF-8/NFC: OK");

# MIE MATCHA Journal Mindful v4.3

Rebuilds the homepage Journal section into a quieter editorial layout and uses the supplied matcha + chasen image as the hero visual.

## Files changed
- `components/home/HomePage.tsx`
- `components/home/HomePage.module.css`
- Adds `public/images/home/journal/journal-mindful-whisk.webp`

## Run
```powershell
cd D:\freetime\Matcha\matcha-archive
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\MIE-MATCHA-JOURNAL-MINDFUL-v4.3\PATCH-JOURNAL-MINDFUL-v4.3.ps1
```

The PowerShell wrapper is ASCII-only. Unicode source is preserved by the Node patcher and normalized to NFC.

================
File: MIE-MATCHA-PRODUCT-MEDIA-CONTRAST-v4.4/PATCH-PRODUCT-MEDIA-CONTRAST-v4.4.ps1
================
param(
  [switch]$SkipChecks
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

function Find-ProjectRoot {
  param([string]$Start)
  $dir = (Resolve-Path $Start).Path
  while ($true) {
    if (Test-Path (Join-Path $dir 'package.json')) { return $dir }
    $parent = Split-Path $dir -Parent
    if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($parent) -or $parent -eq $dir) {
      throw 'Could not locate project root.'
    }
    $dir = $parent
  }
}

$root = Find-ProjectRoot -Start $PSScriptRoot
$cssPath = Join-Path $root 'components\home\HomePage.module.css'
if (-not (Test-Path $cssPath)) {
  throw "Missing file: $cssPath"
}

$stamp = Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss'
$backupDir = Join-Path $root ".backup-product-media-contrast-v4.4-$stamp"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Join-Path $backupDir 'components\home') -Force | Out-Null
Copy-Item -LiteralPath $cssPath -Destination (Join-Path $backupDir 'components\home\HomePage.module.css') -Force

$utf8 = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)
$css = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($cssPath, [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8)

$start = '/* MIE MATCHA PRODUCT MEDIA CONTRAST V4.4 START */'
$end = '/* MIE MATCHA PRODUCT MEDIA CONTRAST V4.4 END */'
$block = @'
/* MIE MATCHA PRODUCT MEDIA CONTRAST V4.4 START */
.productCard {
  background: var(--paper);
}

.productMedia {
  position: relative;
  isolation: isolate;
  background: #d8e3c7;
  border-bottom: 0.5px solid var(--line);
  transition: background-color 220ms ease;
}

.productMedia::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  inset: clamp(0.55rem, 1vw, 0.85rem);
  z-index: 0;
  border: 0.5px solid rgba(59, 44, 25, 0.12);
  pointer-events: none;
}

.productImage {
  z-index: 1;
  object-fit: contain;
  padding: 9%;
  image-rendering: auto;
}

.productCard:hover .productMedia,
.productCard:focus-within .productMedia {
  background: #d1ddb9;
}

@media (max-width: 720px) {
  .productImage {
    padding: 8%;
  }
}
/* MIE MATCHA PRODUCT MEDIA CONTRAST V4.4 END */
'@

$escapedStart = [regex]::Escape($start)
$escapedEnd = [regex]::Escape($end)
$pattern = "$escapedStart[\s\S]*?$escapedEnd"

if ([regex]::IsMatch($css, $pattern)) {
  $css = [regex]::Replace($css, $pattern, $block)
} else {
  $css = $css.TrimEnd() + "`r`n`r`n" + $block + "`r`n"
}

[System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($cssPath, $css.Normalize([Text.NormalizationForm]::FormC), $utf8)

Remove-Item (Join-Path $root '.next') -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
Remove-Item (Join-Path $root 'tsconfig.tsbuildinfo') -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

Write-Host "Project root: $root"
Write-Host "Backup: $backupDir"
Write-Host 'Updated: components/home/HomePage.module.css'
Write-Host 'Product media background: #d8e3c7'
Write-Host 'Product image padding: 9%'

if (-not $SkipChecks) {
  Push-Location $root
  try {
    npm run verify
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'npm run verify failed' }
    npm run typecheck
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'npm run typecheck failed' }
  }
  finally {
    Pop-Location
  }
}

Write-Host 'PRODUCT MEDIA CONTRAST v4.4 COMPLETED.'
